const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const state={lang:'en',view:'home',detail:null,query:'',studyN:null,revealed:false,openChapter:'',loading:{en:false,fr:false},mode:{en:'fallback',fr:'empty'},errors:{},corpora:{en:null,fr:null},sectionFilter:'all'};
const key=n=>'ao_cate_v6_'+n;
const setJSON=(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(key(k),JSON.stringify(v))}catch{}};
const getJSON=(k,d)=>{try{const v=localStorage.getItem(key(k));return v?JSON.parse(v):d}catch{return d}};
const getSet=k=>new Set(getJSON(k,[])); const putSet=(k,s)=>setJSON(k,[...s]);
function langFromApp(){return $('.homeScreen')?.dataset.homeLanguage||$('.liveScreen')?.dataset.language||state.lang||'en'}
function t(en,fr){return state.lang==='fr'?(fr||en):en}
function htmlText(s){const d=document.createElement('div');d.innerHTML=String(s||'');return (d.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function cleanMd(s){return String(s||'').replace(/^#+\s*/gm,'').replace(/\*\*|__/g,'').replace(/\*|_/g,'').replace(/`/g,'').replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^\)]+\)/g,'$1').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function parseEnglish(md,meta){const out=[];const re=/^\*\*(\d+)\.\*\*\s*(.*?)\s*$/gm;const ms=[...md.matchAll(re)];for(let i=0;i<ms.length;i++){const n=Number(ms[i][1]);let q=cleanMd(ms[i][2]);const start=ms[i].index+ms[i][0].length,end=i+1<ms.length?ms[i+1].index:md.length;let block=md.slice(start,end).trim();block=block.replace(/^\s*[-_]{3,}\s*$/gm,'').trim();let a=cleanMd(block);if(!n||!q||!a)continue;out.push({n,q,a,chapter:meta.id,part:meta.part,source:'en-open-study'})}return out}
function parseFrench(txt,meta){const o=JSON.parse(txt),out=[];function walk(v){if(!v)return;if(Array.isArray(v)){for(const x of v)walk(x);return}if(typeof v!=='object')return;if(Array.isArray(v.qa)){for(const x of v.qa){if(!x?.n)continue;const n=Number(x.n),repair=FR_SOURCE_REPAIRS_V416[n];let q=repair?.q||htmlText(x.q),a=repair?.a||htmlText(x.a);const sourceTruncated=!repair&&q&&!/[?？]\s*$/.test(q);if(sourceTruncated)q=q.replace(/\s+$/,'')+' …';out.push({n,q,a,chapter:meta.id,part:meta.part,source:repair?'fr-structured+v416-repair':'fr-structured',sourceTruncated,repairSource:repair?.source||null,repairUrl:repair?.url||null})}}for(const [k,x] of Object.entries(v))if(k!=='qa')walk(x)}walk(o);return out}
async function fetchWithBases(path,bases){let last;for(const b of bases){try{const r=await fetch(b+path,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status);return await r.text()}catch(e){last=e}}throw last||new Error('Source unavailable')}
function validate(items){const m=new Map;for(const x of items){if(x.n>=1&&x.n<=433&&x.q&&x.a)m.set(x.n,x)}const arr=[...m.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.n-b.n);const missing=[];for(let n=1;n<=433;n++)if(!m.has(n))missing.push(n);return {items:arr,missing,full:arr.length===433&&missing.length===0}}
function readCache(lang){const c=getJSON('cache_'+lang,null);if(c?.version===CACHE_VERSION&&Array.isArray(c.items)){const v=validate(c.items);if(v.full){state.corpora[lang]=v.items;state.mode[lang]='cached';return true}}return false}
function writeCache(lang,items){setJSON('cache_'+lang,{version:CACHE_VERSION,savedAt:Date.now(),items});state.mode[lang]='cached'}
function corpus(lang=state.lang){if(state.corpora[lang])return state.corpora[lang];if(lang==='en')return FALLBACK_EN.map(x=>({...x,chapter:x.n<=27?'intro':x.n<=160?'mysteries':x.n<=266?'commandments':x.n<=413?'sacraments':'prayer',part:x.n<=27?'intro':x.n<=160?'faith':x.n<=266?'moral':x.n<=413?'grace':'prayer',source:'offline-core'}));return []}
async function loadFull(lang,force=false){if(state.loading[lang])return;if(!force&&state.corpora[lang]&&state.corpora[lang].length===433)return;state.loading[lang]=true;state.errors[lang]='';render();try{const bases=lang==='fr'?FR_BASES:EN_BASES;const jobs=MANIFEST.map(async meta=>{const path=lang==='fr'?meta.frPath:meta.enPath;const txt=await fetchWithBases(path,bases);return lang==='fr'?parseFrench(txt,meta):parseEnglish(txt,meta)});const chunks=await Promise.all(jobs);const v=validate(chunks.flat());if(!v.full)throw new Error('Corpus validation failed: '+v.items.length+'/433; missing '+v.missing.slice(0,12).join(', '));state.corpora[lang]=v.items;writeCache(lang,v.items)}catch(e){state.errors[lang]=String(e?.message||e);if(lang==='en'&&!state.corpora.en)state.mode.en='fallback';else if(lang==='fr'&&!state.corpora.fr)state.mode.fr='empty'}finally{state.loading[lang]=false;render()}}
async function ensureCurrent(){if(readCache(state.lang)){render();return}if(state.lang==='en')state.mode.en='fallback';await loadFull(state.lang)}
async function saveBoth(){await loadFull('en',true);await loadFull('fr',true)}
function learned(){return getSet('learned')}function bookmarks(){return getSet('bookmarks')}function markLast(n){setJSON('last',Number(n))}function last(){return Number(getJSON('last',1))||1}
function learnedPct(from=1,to=433){const s=learned();let c=0,total=to-from+1;for(let n=from;n<=to;n++)if(s.has(n))c++;return {c,total,p:total?Math.round(c/total*100):0}}
function getQ(n){return corpus().find(x=>x.n===Number(n))}
function chapterMeta(id){return MANIFEST.find(x=>x.id===id)||null}function partMeta(id){return PARTS.find(x=>x.id===id)||PARTS[0]}
function questionFlags(x){const l=learned(),b=bookmarks();return `${l.has(x.n)?'<span class="aoCateMiniMark" title="Learned">✓</span>':''}${b.has(x.n)?'<span class="aoCateMiniMark" title="Bookmarked">★</span>':''}`}
function historicalNote(x){const s=(x.q+' '+x.a).toLowerCase();if(s.includes('limbo')||s.includes('limbes'))return t('Traditional theological hypothesis: limbo is not a defined dogma. The text is preserved as a historical catechetical formulation.','Hypothèse théologique traditionnelle : les limbes ne sont pas un dogme défini. Le texte est conservé comme formulation catéchétique historique.');if((x.n>=218&&x.n<=221)||s.includes('abstinence')||s.includes('abstinence'))return t('The Church’s fasting and abstinence discipline has changed since 1912. Preserve the doctrinal principle, but consult current law for present obligations.','La discipline de l’Église sur le jeûne et l’abstinence a changé depuis 1912. Le principe doctrinal demeure, mais il faut consulter le droit actuel pour les obligations présentes.');if(x.n===226)return t('Rules concerning solemn celebration of marriage in liturgical seasons have changed since 1912.','Les règles concernant la célébration solennelle du mariage selon les temps liturgiques ont changé depuis 1912.');if(x.n===303)return t('Canonical discipline on the timing of infant Baptism is expressed differently in current law.','La discipline canonique concernant le délai du baptême des petits enfants est formulée différemment dans le droit actuel.');if((x.n>=339&&x.n<=340)||((s.includes('midnight')||s.includes('minuit'))&&(s.includes('fast')||s.includes('jeûn'))))return t('Historical Eucharistic-fast discipline. The midnight fast was later modified; current Roman discipline is different.','Discipline historique du jeûne eucharistique. Le jeûne depuis minuit a ensuite été modifié ; la discipline romaine actuelle est différente.');if(x.n===390||s.includes('days of indulgence')||s.includes('jours d’indulgence'))return t('The old numerical “days/years” way of expressing partial indulgences was abolished in the post-conciliar reform of indulgences.','L’ancienne manière d’exprimer les indulgences partielles en « jours/années » a été supprimée lors de la réforme postconciliaire des indulgences.');if(x.n>=393&&x.n<=400)return t('Traditional terminology and some disciplinary details around Extreme Unction and Holy Orders have developed since 1912; distinguish doctrine from later discipline.','La terminologie traditionnelle et certains détails disciplinaires concernant l’Extrême-Onction et l’Ordre ont évolué depuis 1912 ; il faut distinguer la doctrine de la discipline ultérieure.');if(x.n>=408&&x.n<=412)return t('Some canonical or civil-marriage details reflect the 1912 context and are not a statement of current universal law.','Certains détails canoniques ou civils du mariage reflètent le contexte de 1912 et ne constituent pas un énoncé du droit universel actuel.');return ''}
function shell(){let r=$('#ao-cate-root');if(r)return r;r=document.createElement('div');r.id='ao-cate-root';r.className='aoCateShell';r.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(r);return r}
function openCate(opts={}){state.lang=opts.lang||langFromApp();state.view=opts.view||'home';state.detail=opts.question||null;state.revealed=false;document.body.classList.add('aoCateOpen');shell().hidden=false;if(!state.corpora[state.lang])readCache(state.lang);render();ensureCurrent()}
function closeCate(){shell().hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('aoCateOpen')}
function top(){const detail=!!state.detail;return `<header class="aoCateTop"><button data-cate-${detail?'back':'close'} aria-label="${esc(t(detail?'Back':'Close',detail?'Retour':'Fermer'))}">${detail?'←':'×'}</button><div class="aoCateTopTitle"><small>${esc(t('Ad Orientem · formation','Ad Orientem · formation'))}</small><b>${esc(t('Traditional Catechism','Catéchisme traditionnel'))}</b></div><button type="button" class="aoModuleHome" data-app-home aria-label="${esc(t('Return to Home','Retour à l’accueil'))}">⌂</button><div class="aoCateLang"><button data-cate-lang="en" class="${state.lang==='en'?'active':''}">EN</button><button data-cate-lang="fr" class="${state.lang==='fr'?'active':''}">FR</button></div></header>`}
const NAV=[['home','Overview','Accueil'],['browse','Browse','Parcourir'],['study','Study','Étudier'],['search','Search','Rechercher'],['mass','Mass & Sacraments','Messe & Sacrements'],['sources','Sources','Sources']];
function nav(){return `<nav class="aoCateNav">${NAV.map(x=>`<button data-cate-view="${x[0]}" class="${state.view===x[0]?'active':''}">${esc(t(x[1],x[2]))}</button>`).join('')}</nav>`}
function statusLabel(lang=state.lang){if(state.loading[lang])return t('Loading full 433…','Chargement des 433…');const n=corpus(lang).length;if(state.mode[lang]==='cached'&&n===433)return t('Complete · 433 · offline ready','Complet · 433 · hors ligne');if(lang==='en'&&n===FALLBACK_EN.length)return t(`Core offline · ${n}/433`,`Noyau hors ligne · ${n}/433`);if(state.errors[lang])return t('Source unavailable · retry','Source indisponible · réessayer');return `${n}/433`}
function hero(){const lp=learnedPct(),mode=state.mode[state.lang],loading=state.loading[state.lang];return `<section class="aoCateHero"><div class="aoCateKicker">${esc(t('Learn the Faith','Apprendre la Foi'))}</div><h1>${esc(t('Catechism of St Pius X','Catéchisme de Saint Pie X'))}</h1><p>${esc(t('The concise 1912 catechism as a searchable, progressive study tool. Historical disciplinary statements are preserved but clearly distinguished from current law.','Le catéchisme concis de 1912 comme outil d’étude progressif et consultable. Les énoncés disciplinaires historiques sont conservés mais clairement distingués du droit actuel.'))}</p><div class="aoCateStatusRow"><span class="aoCateStatus ${loading?'loading':mode==='cached'?'ok':''}"><i class="aoCateDot"></i>${esc(statusLabel())}</span><button class="aoCateStatus" data-cate-sync>${esc(t('Refresh source','Actualiser la source'))}</button></div><div class="aoCateProgress"><i style="width:${lp.p}%"></i></div><div class="aoCateProgressText">${lp.c}/433 ${esc(t('marked learned','marquées apprises'))}</div></section>`}
function qButton(x){const cm=chapterMeta(x.chapter);return `<button class="aoCateQA" data-cate-q="${x.n}"><span class="aoCateNum">${x.n}</span><span><b>${esc(x.q)}</b><small>${esc(cm?(state.lang==='fr'?cm.fr:cm.en):t('Catechism question','Question du catéchisme'))}</small></span><span class="aoCateQFlags">${questionFlags(x)}<span>›</span></span></button>`}

questions'))}</p></div><div class="aoCateGrid">${PARTS.map(p=>{const pr=learnedPct(p.from,p.to);return `<button class="aoCatePartCard" data-cate-view="browse" data-cate-part="${p.id}"><em>Q ${p.from}–${p.to}</em><b>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?p.fr:p.en)}</b><span>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?p.desc_fr:p.desc)}</span><div class="aoCatePartProgress"><i favoris'))}</button></div></section>`}
function renderBrowse(){const c=corpus();if(!c.length)return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('French is not cached on this device yet. Connect once and tap Refresh source.','Le français n’est pas encore en cache sur cet appareil. Connectez-vous une fois et touchez Actualiser la source.'))}</div>`;const filtered=state.sectionFilter==='bookmarks'?c.filter(x=>bookmarks().has(x.n)):state.sectionFilter==='learned'?c.filter(x=>learned().has(x.n)):c;const by=new Map;for(const m of MANIFEST){const items=filtered.filter(x=>x.chapter===m.id);if(items.length)by.set(m.id,items)}return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateToolbar"><button data-cate-filter="all" class="${state.sectionFilter==='all'?'active':''}">${esc(t('All','Tout'))}</button><button data-cate-filter="bookmarks" class="${state.sectionFilter==='bookmarks'?'active':''}">${esc(t('Bookmarked','Favoris'))}</button><button data-cate-filter="learned" class="${state.sectionFilter==='learned'?'active':''}">${esc(t('Learned','Apprises'))}</button></div>${MANIFEST.map(m=>{const items=by.get(m.id)||[];if(!items.length)return'';const open=state.openChapter===m.id;const range=`${items[0].n}–${items[items.length-1].n}`;return `<section class="aoCateChapter"><button data-cate-chapter="${m.id}"><b>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?m.fr:m.en)}</b><span>Q ${range} · ${items.length} ${open?'−':'+'}</span></button><div class="aoCateChapterBody" ${open?'':'hidden'}><div class="aoCateQAList">${items.map(qButton).join('')}</div></div></section>`}).join('')||`<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('No questions in this filter.','Aucune question dans ce filtre.'))}</div>`}`}
function chooseStudy(){const c=corpus();if(!c.length)return null;const l=learned(),pool=c.filter(x=>!l.has(x.n));const src=pool.length?pool:c;return src[Math.floor(Math.random()*src.length)]}
function renderStudy(){let x=state.studyN&&getQ(state.studyN);if(!x){x=chooseStudy();state.studyN=x?.n||null;state.revealed=false}if(!x)return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('Load the complete corpus to begin study mode.','Chargez le corpus complet pour commencer le mode étude.'))}</div>`;return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateStudy"><article class="aoCateStudyCard"><div class="number">Q ${x.n} / 433</div><h2>${esc(x.q)}</h2>${state.revealed?`<div class="aoCateStudyAnswer">${esc(x.a)}</div>`:`<button class="aoCateActions aoCateReveal" data-cate-reveal>${esc(t('Reveal answer','Afficher la réponse'))}</button>`}</article><div class="aoCateActions">${state.revealed?`<button data-cate-learn="${x.n}" class="primary">${learned().has(x.n)?esc(t('Learned ✓','Apprise ✓')):esc(t('Mark learned','Marquer apprise'))}</button>`:''}<button data-cate-next-study>${esc(t('Next question','Question suivante'))} →</button><button data-cate-q="${x.n}">${esc(t('Open full card','Ouvrir la fiche'))}</button></div></div>`}
function searchHits(q){const s=q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();if(!s)return[];const c=corpus();return c.filter(x=>String(x.n)===s||x.q.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(s)||x.a.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(s)).slice(0,120)}
function renderSearch(){const hits=searchHits(state.query);return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateSearchBox"><div class="aoCateSearch"><input id="aoCateSearchInput" value="${esc(state.query)}" placeholder="${esc(t('Search question, answer or number…','Rechercher une question, une réponse ou un numéro…'))}" autocomplete="off" inputmode="search"><button data-cate-clear aria-label="${esc(t('Clear','Effacer'))}">×</button></div><div class="aoCateResultCount">${state.query?`${hits.length} ${esc(t('matches','résultats'))}`:esc(t('Search across the complete current-language corpus.','Recherchez dans tout le corpus de la langue actuelle.'))}</div></div>${state.query?`<div class="aoCateQAList">${hits.length?hits.map(qButton).join(''):`<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('No match.','Aucun résultat.'))}</div>`}</div>`:''}`}
function renderMass(){const c=corpus(),euch=c.filter(x=>MASS_RANGE.has(x.n)),groups=MANIFEST.filter(m=>m.part==='grace'&&m.id!=='eucharist').map(m=>({m,items:c.filter(x=>x.chapter===m.id)})).filter(g=>g.items.length);return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateNotice"><b>${esc(t('Doctrine ↔ liturgy','Doctrine ↔ liturgie'))}</b><p>${esc(t('Use the catechism for the doctrinal foundation; use Understand the Mass for the rite, ceremonial, history and the reason for particular actions.','Utilisez le catéchisme pour le fondement doctrinal ; utilisez Comprendre la Messe pour le rite, le cérémonial, l’histoire et la raison des actions particulières.'))}</p><div class="aoCateActions"><button data-open-mass-learn class="primary">${esc(t('Open Understand the Mass','Ouvrir Comprendre la Messe'))}</button></div></div><div class="aoCateSectionTitle"><h2>${esc(t('Eucharist & Holy Mass','Eucharistie & Sainte Messe'))}</h2><p>Q 316–354</p></div><div class="aoCateQAList">${euch.map(qButton).join('')}</div><div class="aoCateSectionTitle"><h2>${esc(t('Other sacraments','Autres sacrements'))}</h2><p>${groups.reduce((n,g)=>n+g.items.length,0)} ${esc(t('questions','questions'))}</p></div>${groups.map(({m,items})=>{const open=state.openChapter===m.id;return `<section class="aoCateChapter"><button data-cate-chapter="${m.id}"><b>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?m.fr:m.en)}</b><span>Q ${items[0].n}–${items[items.length-1].n} · ${open?'−':'+'}</span></button><div class="aoCateChapterBody" ${open?'':'hidden'}><div class="aoCateQAList">${items.map(qButton).join('')}</div></div></section>`}).join('')}`}
function sourceCard(s){return `<article class="aoCateSource"><span class="kind">${esc(state.lang==='fr'?s.kind_fr:s.kind)}</span><b>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?s.label_fr:s.label)}</b><p>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?s.note_fr:s.note)}</p><a href="${esc(s.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(t('Open source ↗','Ouvrir la source ↗'))}</a></article>`}
function renderSources(){return `${hero()}${nav()}<div class="aoCateNotice"><b>${esc(t('What is stored in the HTML?','Qu’est-ce qui est stocké dans le HTML ?'))}</b><p>${esc(t(`Only a ${FALLBACK_EN.length}-question verified English core is embedded as a first-run fallback. The complete 433-question language corpus is fetched from the structured source and cached locally after a successful load. This keeps the single-file app small while allowing complete offline use after the first sync.`,`Seul un noyau anglais vérifié de ${FALLBACK_EN.length} questions est intégré comme solution de secours au premier lancement. Le corpus complet de 433 questions de chaque langue est chargé depuis la source structurée puis mis en cache localement. Cela garde l’application monofichier légère tout en permettant un usage hors ligne complet après la première synchronisation.`))}</p></div>${SOURCE_CARDS.map(sourceCard).join('')}`}
function renderDetail(n){const x=getQ(n);if(!x)return `<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('This question is not available in the current local corpus yet. Refresh the source.','Cette question n’est pas encore disponible dans le corpus local actuel. Actualisez la source.'))}</div>`;markLast(x.n);const cm=chapterMeta(x.chapter),pm=partMeta(x.part),note=historicalNote(x),l=learned(),b=bookmarks(),prev=getQ(x.n-1),next=getQ(x.n+1),mass=MASS_RANGE.has(x.n);return `<article class="aoCateArticle"><div class="aoCateArticleHead"><div class="aoCateKicker">${esc(t('Question','Question'))} ${x.n} / 433</div><div class="aoCateMetaLine"><span>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?pm.fr:pm.en)}</span>${cm?`<span>· ${esc(state.lang==='fr'?cm.fr:cm.en)}</span>`:''}</div><h1>${esc(x.q)}</h1></div><div class="aoCateAnswer"><p>${esc(x.a)}</p></div>${x.sourceTruncated?`<div class="aoCateFlag"><b>${esc(t('Source-transcription note','Note sur la transcription source'))}</b><span>${esc(t('The structured French source truncates this question line at a historical page break. The source answer is preserved; consult the linked historical edition for the complete printed question.','La source française structurée tronque cette ligne de question à un saut de page historique. La réponse source est conservée ; consultez l’édition historique liée pour la question imprimée complète.'))}</span></div>`:''}${note?`<div class="aoCateFlag"><b>${esc(t('Historical context / discipline','Contexte / discipline historique'))}</b><span>${esc(note)}</span></div>`:''}<div class="aoCateActions"><button data-cate-learn="${x.n}" class="${l.has(x.n)?'primary':''}">${l.has(x.n)?'✓ '+esc(t('Learned','Apprise')):esc(t('Mark learned','Marquer apprise'))}</button><button data-cate-bookmark="${x.n}" class="${b.has(x.n)?'primary':''}">${b.has(x.n)?'★ '+esc(t('Bookmarked','Favori')):'☆ '+esc(t('Bookmark','Favori'))}</button>${mass?`<button data-open-mass-learn>${esc(t('Study in Understand the Mass','Étudier dans Comprendre la Messe'))}</button>`:''}</div><div class="aoCatePager">${prev?`<button data-cate-q="${prev.n}">← Q${prev.n}<br><small>${esc(prev.q)}</small></button>`:'<span></span>'}${next?`<button data-cate-q="${next.n}">Q${next.n} →<br><small>${esc(next.q)}</small></button>`:''}</div><div class="aoCateNotice"><b>${esc(t('Text provenance','Provenance du texte'))}</b><p>${esc(state.lang==='fr'?t('','Texte français chargé depuis la transcription structurée du Petit Catéchisme de 1912 ; voir Sources pour la référence historique Bailly 1913.'):'English study translation aligned to the canonical 1912 Italian text; it is not labelled as the historical Hagan edition. See Sources for provenance.')}</p></div></article>`}
function render(){const r=shell();let body;if(state.detail)body=renderDetail(state.detail);else body=state.view==='home'?renderOverview():state.view==='browse'?renderBrowse():state.view==='study'?renderStudy():state.view==='search'?renderSearch():state.view==='mass'?renderMass():renderSources();r.innerHTML=`${top()}<div class="aoCateWrap">${body}</div>`;if(!state.detail&&state.view!=='search')r.scrollTop=0;if(state.view==='search'&&!state.detail)setTimeout(()=>$('#aoCateSearchInput',r)?.focus(),0);enhanceUnderstand()}
function setView(v,part=''){state.view=v;state.detail=null;state.revealed=false;if(v==='browse'&&part){state.sectionFilter='all';const first=MANIFEST.find(x=>x.part===part);state.openChapter=first?.id||''}render()}
function setLang(lang){state.lang=lang;state.detail=null;state.studyN=null;state.revealed=false;if(!state.corpora[lang])readCache(lang);render();ensureCurrent()}
function enhanceUnderstand(){const root=$('#ao-learn-root');if(!root||root.hidden)return;const wrap=$('.aoLearnWrap',root);if(!wrap)return;const hero=$('.aoLearnHero',wrap);if(!hero)return;const fr=$('[data-learn-lang=\"fr\"]',root)?.classList.contains('active');const lt=(en,frText)=>fr?(frText||en):en;if(!$('.aoFormationBridge',wrap)){const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='aoFormationBridge';d.innerHTML=`<div><small>${esc(lt('Formation companion','Compagnon de formation'))}</small><b>${esc(lt('Traditional Catechism','Catéchisme traditionnel'))}</b><p>${esc(lt('Move from the rite to the doctrine behind the sacraments, Eucharist and Christian life.','Passez du rite à la doctrine sur les sacrements, l’Eucharistie et la vie chrétienne.'))}</p></div><button data-open-catechism aria-label="${esc(lt('Open Catechism','Ouvrir le Catéchisme'))}">→</button>`;hero.after(d)}if($('.aoLessonList',wrap)&&!$('.aoFormationProgress',wrap)){let done=[];try{done=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ao_understand_mass_progress_v1')||'[]')}catch{}const ids=['mass','read','space','foot','entrance','word','offertory','canon','consecration','communion','end','forms','year','exceptional'];const first=ids.find(id=>!done.includes(id))||ids[0];const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='aoFormationProgress';box.innerHTML=`<div class="aoFormationProgressHead"><b>${esc(lt('Course progress','Progression du parcours'))}</b><span>${done.length}/14</span></div><div class="aoCateProgress"><i style="width:${Math.round(done.length/14*100)}%"></i></div><button data-learn-lesson="${first}">${esc(done.length===14?lt('Review the course','Revoir le parcours'):lt('Continue course','Continuer le parcours'))} →</button>`;$('.aoFormationBridge',wrap).after(box)}}
document.addEventListener('click',async e=>{const el=e.target.closest('button,a');if(!el)return;if(el.closest('#ao-cate-root')){if(el.matches('[data-cate-close]')){closeCate();return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-back]')){state.detail=null;render();return}if(el.dataset.cateReset){const k=el.dataset.cateReset,msg=k==='learned'?t('Clear all learned marks?','Effacer toutes les marques apprises ?'):t('Clear all bookmarks?','Effacer tous les favoris ?');if(confirm(msg)){putSet(k,new Set());render()}return}if(el.dataset.cateLang){setLang(el.dataset.cateLang);return}if(el.dataset.cateQ){state.detail=Number(el.dataset.cateQ);state.revealed=false;render();return}if(el.dataset.cateView){setView(el.dataset.cateView,el.dataset.catePart||'');return}if(el.dataset.cateChapter){state.openChapter=state.openChapter===el.dataset.cateChapter?'':el.dataset.cateChapter;render();return}if(el.dataset.cateFilter){state.sectionFilter=el.dataset.cateFilter;render();return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-sync]')){loadFull(state.lang,true);return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-offline]')){el.disabled=true;el.textContent=t('Saving both languages…','Enregistrement des deux langues…');await saveBoth();render();return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-reveal]')){state.revealed=true;render();return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-next-study]')){const x=chooseStudy();state.studyN=x?.n||null;state.revealed=false;render();return}if(el.dataset.cateLearn){const s=learned(),n=Number(el.dataset.cateLearn);s.has(n)?s.delete(n):s.add(n);putSet('learned',s);render();return}if(el.dataset.cateBookmark){const s=bookmarks(),n=Number(el.dataset.cateBookmark);s.has(n)?s.delete(n):s.add(n);putSet('bookmarks',s);render();return}if(el.matches('[data-cate-clear]')){state.query='';render();return}if(el.matches('[data-open-mass-learn]')){closeCate();window.AO_UNDERSTAND_MASS?.open?.();return}}if(el.matches('[data-open-catechism]')){window.AO_UNDERSTAND_MASS?.close?.();openCate();return}})
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target?.id==='aoCateSearchInput'){state.query=e.target.value;const box=$('.aoCateWrap',shell());const hits=searchHits(state.query);const list=box?.querySelector('.aoCateQAList');const count=box?.querySelector('.aoCateResultCount');if(count)count.textContent=state.query?(hits.length+' '+t('matches','résultats')):t('Search across the complete current-language corpus.','Recherchez dans tout le corpus de la langue actuelle.');if(list)list.innerHTML=hits.length?hits.map(qButton).join(''):`<div class="aoCateEmpty">${esc(t('No match.','Aucun résultat.'))}</div>`}})
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{const r=$('#ao-cate-root');if(e.key==='Escape'&&r&&!r.hidden){state.detail?(state.detail=null,render()):closeCate()}})
readCache('en');readCache('fr');
window.AO_TRADITIONAL_CATECHISM={open:openCate,close:closeCate,back:()=>{if(state.detail){state.detail=null;render();return true}closeCate();return true},resetLearned:()=>{putSet('learned',new Set());render();return true},resetBookmarks:()=>{putSet('bookmarks',new Set());render();return true},openQuestion:(n)=>{const x=getQ(n);if(!x)return false;state.detail=Number(n);state.revealed=false;render();return true},restoreState:(x={})=>{state.lang=x.lang||langFromApp();state.view=x.view||'home';state.detail=x.detail??null;state.query=x.query||'';state.revealed=!!x.revealed;document.body.classList.add('aoCateOpen');shell().hidden=false;render();return true},getState:()=>({...state}),load:(lang='en')=>loadFull(lang,true),count:()=>corpus().length,enhanceUnderstand,version:CACHE_VERSION};
})();
</script>

<script id="ao-content-audit-v14-js">
(()=>{'use strict';
const STATIC={sourceFiles:{latin:1066,en:945,fr:952,both:930},temporal:{latin:526,both:475},sanctoral:{latin:524,both:448},commune:{liveLatin:2,liveEn:2,liveFr:1,normalized1962:28,addressableLa:28,addressableEn:28,addressableFr:28},fixed:{what:118,understand:118,history:12,massAdjacentPrayerUnits:26}};
const FIELD_LABELS={introit:'Introit',collects:'Collect(s)',epistle:'Epistle / Lesson',gradual:'Gradual / Tract / Alleluia',sequence:'Sequence',gospel:'Gospel',offertory:'Offertory',secrets:'Secret(s)',preface:'Preface',communion:'Communion',postcommunions:'Postcommunion(s)',preparatoryLessons:'Preparatory lessons',preparatoryGraduals:'Preparatory graduals',preparatoryCollects:'Preparatory collects'};
const CATEGORY_ORDER=['Temporal','Sanctoral','Requiem','Common','Other'];
let lastReport=null,running=false,activePanel='summary';
const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[c]));
const iso=d=>`${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
const pct=(a,b)=>b?Math.round(a/b*1000)/10:0;
function eachText(p){const arr=[];const add=(slot,v)=>{if(!v)return;if(typeof v==='string'&&v.trim()){arr.push({slot,text:{lat:v,en:v,fr:v}});return}if(typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)&&(String(v.lat||'').trim()||String(v.en||'').trim()||String(v.fr||'').trim()))arr.push({slot,text:v})};add('introit',p.introit);(p.collects||[]).forEach(v=>add('collects',v));add('epistle',p.epistle);add('gradual',p.gradual);add('sequence',p.sequence);add('gospel',p.gospel);add('offertory',p.offertory);(p.secrets||[]).forEach(v=>add('secrets',v));add('preface',p.preface);add('communion',p.communion);(p.postcommunions||[]).forEach(v=>add('postcommunions',v));for(const row of (p.preparatoryLessons||[])){add('preparatoryLessons',row.lesson);add('preparatoryGraduals',row.gradual);add('preparatoryCollects',row.collect)}return arr}
function kindFor(slot){return slot==='gospel'?'gospel':'scripture'}
function baseCategory(p){const path=String(p?.sourcePath||'');if(p?.isRequiem||p?.riteProfile==='requiem_mass_1962'||/^Sancti\/11-02m/.test(path))return'Requiem';if(path.startsWith('Tempora/'))return'Temporal';if(path.startsWith('Sancti/'))return'Sanctoral';if(path.startsWith('Commune/'))return'Common';return'Other'}
function isExceptional(p){return !!p?.riteProfile && p.riteProfile!=='ordinary_mass' && p.riteProfile!=='requiem_mass_1962'}
function blankCategory(name){return{name,days:0,formularies:new Set(),en:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},fr:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},missing:0,commentary:{references:0,enFull:0,frIntegrated:0,frLinkOnly:0,frMissing:0}}}
function blankField(name){return{name,en:{expected:0,available:0,missing:0},fr:{expected:0,available:0,missing:0},dates:new Set()}}
function coverageForField(p,lang,field){const c=p?.languageCoverage?.[lang];if(!c)return null;const missing=new Set(c.missing||[]);const aliases=[field,FIELD_LABELS[field],field==='collects'?'Collects':null,field==='secrets'?'Secrets':null,field==='postcommunions'?'Postcommunions':null].filter(Boolean);const miss=aliases.some(x=>missing.has(x));return{expected:true,available:!miss}}
function noteFieldStats(report,p,date){const units=eachText(p).filter(x=>String(x.text?.lat||'').trim());for(const u of units){const fs=report.fields[u.slot]||(report.fields[u.slot]=blankField(u.slot));fs.dates.add(date);for(const lang of ['en','fr']){fs[lang].expected++;if(String(u.text?.[lang]||'').trim())fs[lang].available++;else fs[lang].missing++}}}
function commentaryBucket(report,category){return report.categories[category]?.commentary||report.categories.Other.commentary}
function commentaryStatus(doc){const docs=doc?.parts?.length?doc.parts:[doc];if(!docs.length||!docs[0])return{parsed:false,en:false,fr:'missing',scope:null};const parsed=docs.every(x=>!!x?.reference);if(!parsed)return{parsed:false,en:false,fr:'missing',scope:null};const en=docs.every(x=>x.reference?.kind==='gospel'?!!x.englishCatena?.length:(!!x.englishCommentary?.entries?.length||!!x.englishNotes?.length));const frInt=docs.every(x=>!!x.frenchCommentary?.entries?.length||!!x.frenchCatena?.length);const frLink=!frInt&&docs.every(x=>x.reference?.kind==='gospel'&&!!x.frenchCatenaUrl);return{parsed:true,en,fr:frInt?'integrated':frLink?'link-only':'missing',scope:docs.map(x=>x.frenchCommentary?.scope).filter(Boolean).join('+')||null}}
async function auditYear(year,{verifyCommentary=true,onProgress=()=>{}}={}){
 const rt=window.AO_RUNTIME_V8;if(!rt?.resolver)throw new Error('Ad Orientem runtime is not ready.');
 const report={version:'14',year,generated:new Date().toISOString(),scope:'calendar-resolved General Roman Calendar plus source-tree supplementary gate',days:{attempted:0,resolved:0,failed:0,properReady:0,exceptional:0,inherited:0},proper:{en:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},fr:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},missing:[],uniqueFormularies:[]},categories:{},fields:{},exceptional:{profiles:{},items:[]},commentary:{references:0,attempted:0,parseOrLoadFailures:0,en:{full:0,missing:0},fr:{integrated:0,psalmLevel:0,verseNotes:0,linkOnly:0,missing:0},items:[],failures:[],bySlot:{}},sources:{structuralInheritances:0,legacyCommonRecoveries:0,structuralExamples:[],legacyCommonExamples:[]},static:STATIC,supplementary:{commune:{normalized1962Files:28,recoveryChain:'normalized same-language -> current missa -> pinned legacy missa Commune -> horas; normalized Latin reference structure inherited without Latin prose',legacyMassFiles:{la:48,en:40,fr:47},knownSpecialCase:'C7a French has no legacy Mass file; current same-language horas supplies its Mass sections and normalized Latin contributes only missing reference structure; runtime audit remains controlling',status:'Common references now recovered from genuine same-language historical Mass sources where available; runtime field audit remains controlling'},votive:{status:'enumerated separately by v15 supplementary gate',normalizedFiles:21,seasonalMarianCommonForms:5},requiem:{status:'daily Requiem plus all three All Souls Masses explicitly enumerated by v15'}},errors:[]};
 for(const n of CATEGORY_ORDER)report.categories[n]=blankCategory(n);
 const forms=new Map(),refs=new Map();const start=new Date(`${year}-01-01T12:00:00`),end=new Date(`${year+1}-01-01T12:00:00`),total=Math.round((end-start)/86400000);let idx=0;
 for(let d=new Date(start);d<end;d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)){
   idx++;const date=iso(d);report.days.attempted++;onProgress(idx/total*.62,`Calendar ${idx}/${total} · ${date}`);
   try{
     const res=await rt.resolver.resolveDay(date);if(res?.status!=='ready'){report.days.failed++;report.errors.push({date,stage:'calendar',error:res?.error||'day resolution failed'});continue}report.days.resolved++;
     if(res.proper?.status!=='ready'||!res.proper.data){report.days.failed++;report.errors.push({date,stage:'proper',error:res.proper?.error||'Proper unavailable'});continue}
     const diag=res.diagnostic||{};const structural=diag.structuralInheritances||[],legacy=diag.legacyCommonRecoveries||[];report.sources.structuralInheritances+=structural.length;report.sources.legacyCommonRecoveries+=legacy.length;for(const x of structural){if(report.sources.structuralExamples.length<80)report.sources.structuralExamples.push({date,...x})}for(const x of legacy){if(report.sources.legacyCommonExamples.length<80)report.sources.legacyCommonExamples.push({date,...x})}
     report.days.properReady++;const p=res.proper.data,category=baseCategory(p),cat=report.categories[category]||report.categories.Other;cat.days++;if(p.inheritedProper)report.days.inherited++;
     const key=p.sourcePath||p.properId||`${date}:${p.title?.la||''}`;cat.formularies.add(key);if(!forms.has(key))forms.set(key,{key,category,properId:p.properId||null,sourcePath:p.sourcePath||null,title:p.title?.en||p.title?.fr||p.title?.la||'',riteProfile:p.riteProfile||'ordinary_mass',dates:[]});forms.get(key).dates.push(date);
     if(isExceptional(p)){report.days.exceptional++;const pr=p.riteProfile||'exceptional';report.exceptional.profiles[pr]=(report.exceptional.profiles[pr]||0)+1;report.exceptional.items.push({date,profile:pr,path:p.sourcePath||'',title:p.title?.en||p.title?.la||''})}
     for(const lang of ['en','fr']){const c=p.languageCoverage?.[lang];if(c){report.proper[lang].expected+=c.expected||0;report.proper[lang].available+=c.available||0;cat[lang].expected+=c.expected||0;cat[lang].available+=c.available||0;if(c.complete){report.proper[lang].completeDates++;cat[lang].completeDates++}else{cat.missing++;report.proper.missing.push({date,category,lang,properId:p.properId||'',sourcePath:p.sourcePath||'',fields:c.missing||[]})}}}
     noteFieldStats(report,p,date);
     for(const x of eachText(p)){const normText=v=>String(v||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();const id=[x.slot,normText(x.text?.lat),normText(x.text?.en),normText(x.text?.fr)].join('|');if(!refs.has(id))refs.set(id,{text:x.text,slot:x.slot,dates:[date],categories:new Set([category]),paths:new Set([p.sourcePath||''])});else{const z=refs.get(id);z.dates.push(date);z.categories.add(category);z.paths.add(p.sourcePath||'')}}
   }catch(e){report.days.failed++;report.errors.push({date,stage:'exception',error:e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e)})}
 }
 report.proper.uniqueFormularies=[...forms.values()];
 if(verifyCommentary){const list=[...refs.values()];let ci=0;for(const item of list){ci++;report.commentary.attempted++;onProgress(.62+(ci/Math.max(1,list.length))*.37,`Commentary ${ci}/${list.length}`);let doc=null;try{doc=await rt.scriptureService.load(kindFor(item.slot),item.text)}catch(e){report.commentary.parseOrLoadFailures++;report.commentary.failures.push({slot:item.slot,dates:item.dates.slice(0,12),paths:[...item.paths].slice(0,8),error:e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e)});continue}const st=commentaryStatus(doc);if(!st.parsed){report.commentary.parseOrLoadFailures++;report.commentary.failures.push({slot:item.slot,dates:item.dates.slice(0,12),paths:[...item.paths].slice(0,8),error:'No parsed Scripture reference'});continue}report.commentary.references++;if(!report.commentary.bySlot[item.slot])report.commentary.bySlot[item.slot]={references:0,enFull:0,frIntegrated:0,frLinkOnly:0,frMissing:0};const slot=report.commentary.bySlot[item.slot];slot.references++;if(st.en){report.commentary.en.full++;slot.enFull++}else report.commentary.en.missing++;if(st.fr==='integrated'){report.commentary.fr.integrated++;slot.frIntegrated++;const docs=doc.parts?.length?doc.parts:[doc];if(docs.some(x=>x.frenchCommentary?.scope==='psalm-full'))report.commentary.fr.psalmLevel++;if(docs.some(x=>x.frenchCommentary?.scope==='verse-notes'))report.commentary.fr.verseNotes++}else if(st.fr==='link-only'){report.commentary.fr.linkOnly++;slot.frLinkOnly++}else{report.commentary.fr.missing++;slot.frMissing++}
     for(const category of item.categories){const b=commentaryBucket(report,category);b.references++;if(st.en)b.enFull++;if(st.fr==='integrated')b.frIntegrated++;else if(st.fr==='link-only')b.frLinkOnly++;else b.frMissing++}
     report.commentary.items.push({reference:doc.reference?.display||doc.parts?.[0]?.reference?.display||'',referenceFr:doc.reference?.displayFr||doc.parts?.[0]?.reference?.displayFr||'',kind:doc.reference?.kind||doc.parts?.[0]?.reference?.kind||'',slot:item.slot,en:st.en?'full':'missing',fr:st.fr,frScope:st.scope,dates:item.dates.slice(0,12),categories:[...item.categories],paths:[...item.paths].slice(0,8)})
   }}
 for(const c of Object.values(report.categories))c.formularies=[...c.formularies];for(const f of Object.values(report.fields))f.dates=[...f.dates];onProgress(1,'Audit complete.');return report
}
function mergeReports(reports){if(reports.length===1)return reports[0];const base={version:'14',matrix:true,years:reports.map(r=>r.year),generated:new Date().toISOString(),scope:'multi-year calendar-resolved certification matrix',days:{attempted:0,resolved:0,failed:0,properReady:0,exceptional:0,inherited:0},proper:{en:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},fr:{expected:0,available:0,completeDates:0},missing:[],uniqueFormularies:[]},categories:{},fields:{},exceptional:{profiles:{},items:[]},commentary:{references:0,attempted:0,parseOrLoadFailures:0,en:{full:0,missing:0},fr:{integrated:0,psalmLevel:0,verseNotes:0,linkOnly:0,missing:0},items:[],failures:[],bySlot:{}},sources:{structuralInheritances:0,legacyCommonRecoveries:0,structuralExamples:[],legacyCommonExamples:[]},static:STATIC,supplementary:reports[0].supplementary,errors:[]};for(const n of CATEGORY_ORDER)base.categories[n]=blankCategory(n);const forms=new Map(),commentaryKeys=new Set();
 for(const r of reports){base.sources.structuralInheritances+=r.sources?.structuralInheritances||0;base.sources.legacyCommonRecoveries+=r.sources?.legacyCommonRecoveries||0;for(const x of (r.sources?.structuralExamples||[])){if(base.sources.structuralExamples.length<120)base.sources.structuralExamples.push({...x,year:r.year})}for(const x of (r.sources?.legacyCommonExamples||[])){if(base.sources.legacyCommonExamples.length<120)base.sources.legacyCommonExamples.push({...x,year:r.year})}for(const k of Object.keys(base.days))base.days[k]+=r.days[k]||0;for(const lang of ['en','fr'])for(const k of ['expected','available','completeDates'])base.proper[lang][k]+=r.proper[lang][k]||0;base.proper.missing.push(...r.proper.missing.map(x=>({...x,year:r.year})));r.proper.uniqueFormularies.forEach(x=>{const k=x.key||x.sourcePath||x.properId;if(!forms.has(k))forms.set(k,{...x,dates:[]});forms.get(k).dates.push(...x.dates)});base.errors.push(...r.errors.map(x=>({...x,year:r.year})));base.exceptional.items.push(...r.exceptional.items.map(x=>({...x,year:r.year})));for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(r.exceptional.profiles))base.exceptional.profiles[k]=(base.exceptional.profiles[k]||0)+v;
  for(const name of CATEGORY_ORDER){const a=base.categories[name],b=r.categories[name];a.days+=b.days;a.missing+=b.missing;b.formularies.forEach(x=>a.formularies.add(x));for(const lang of ['en','fr'])for(const k of ['expected','available','completeDates'])a[lang][k]+=b[lang][k]||0;for(const k of Object.keys(a.commentary))a.commentary[k]+=b.commentary[k]||0}
  for(const [field,b] of Object.entries(r.fields)){const a=base.fields[field]||(base.fields[field]=blankField(field));for(const lang of ['en','fr'])for(const k of ['expected','available','missing'])a[lang][k]+=b[lang][k]||0;b.dates.forEach(x=>a.dates.add(`${r.year}:${x}`))}
  base.commentary.attempted+=r.commentary.attempted;base.commentary.parseOrLoadFailures+=r.commentary.parseOrLoadFailures;base.commentary.failures.push(...r.commentary.failures.map(x=>({...x,year:r.year})));for(const lang of ['en'])for(const k of ['full','missing'])base.commentary[lang][k]+=r.commentary[lang][k]||0;for(const k of ['integrated','psalmLevel','verseNotes','linkOnly','missing'])base.commentary.fr[k]+=r.commentary.fr[k]||0;for(const [slot,b] of Object.entries(r.commentary.bySlot)){const a=base.commentary.bySlot[slot]||(base.commentary.bySlot[slot]={references:0,enFull:0,frIntegrated:0,frLinkOnly:0,frMissing:0});for(const k of Object.keys(a))a[k]+=b[k]||0}for(const x of r.commentary.items){const k=[x.slot,x.reference,x.en,x.fr,x.frScope].join('|');if(!commentaryKeys.has(k)){commentaryKeys.add(k);base.commentary.items.push({...x,years:[r.year]})}else{const hit=base.commentary.items.find(y=>[y.slot,y.reference,y.en,y.fr,y.frScope].join('|')===k);if(hit&&!hit.years.includes(r.year))hit.years.push(r.year)}} }
 base.proper.uniqueFormularies=[...forms.values()];base.commentary.references=base.commentary.items.length;for(const c of Object.values(base.categories))c.formularies=[...c.formularies];for(const f of Object.values(base.fields))f.dates=[...f.dates];return base}
function progress(r,msg){const o=document.getElementById('ao-content-audit-v14');if(!o)return;const bar=o.querySelector('[data-auditbar]'),st=o.querySelector('[data-auditstatus]');if(bar)bar.style.width=`${Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,r))*100)}%`;if(st)st.textContent=msg||''}
function badge(text,cls=''){return`<span class="aoAuditBadge ${cls}">${esc(text)}</span>`}
function categoryRows(r){return CATEGORY_ORDER.map(n=>{const c=r.categories[n],en=pct(c.en.available,c.en.expected),fr=pct(c.fr.available,c.fr.expected),fc=pct(c.commentary.frIntegrated,c.commentary.references);return`<tr><td><b>${esc(n)}</b></td><td>${c.days}</td><td>${c.formularies.length}</td><td>${en}%</td><td>${fr}%</td><td>${c.commentary.references?fc+'%':'—'}</td></tr>`}).join('')}
function fieldRows(r){return Object.entries(r.fields).map(([k,f])=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(FIELD_LABELS[k]||k)}</b></td><td>${f.en.available}/${f.en.expected}</td><td>${f.en.missing}</td><td>${f.fr.available}/${f.fr.expected}</td><td>${f.fr.missing}</td></tr>`).join('')}
function slotRows(r){return Object.entries(r.commentary.bySlot).sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).map(([k,x])=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(FIELD_LABELS[k]||k)}</b></td><td>${x.references}</td><td>${pct(x.enFull,x.references)}%</td><td>${pct(x.frIntegrated,x.references)}%</td><td>${x.frLinkOnly}</td><td>${x.frMissing}</td></tr>`).join('')}
function renderSummary(r){const enp=pct(r.proper.en.available,r.proper.en.expected),frp=pct(r.proper.fr.available,r.proper.fr.expected),enC=pct(r.commentary.en.full,r.commentary.references),frC=pct(r.commentary.fr.integrated,r.commentary.references),src=r.sources||{structuralInheritances:0,legacyCommonRecoveries:0,structuralExamples:[],legacyCommonExamples:[]};return`<div class="aoAuditMatrix"><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${enp}%</b><span>EN Proper fields</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${frp}%</b><span>FR Proper fields</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${enC}%</b><span>EN commentary</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${frC}%</b><span>FR commentary</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${r.proper.uniqueFormularies.length}</b><span>unique resolved formularies</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${r.commentary.parseOrLoadFailures}</b><span>Scripture parse/load failures</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${src.structuralInheritances}</b><span>vernacular structural inheritances</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${src.legacyCommonRecoveries}</b><span>legacy Mass Common recoveries</span></div></div><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Certification scope</h3><div>${badge(`${r.days.properReady}/${r.days.attempted} calendar days`,r.days.failed?'warn':'ok')}${badge(`${r.days.exceptional} exceptional-day resolutions`)}${badge(`${r.days.inherited} inherited feria resolutions`)}${badge(r.matrix?`${r.years.length} test years`:`year ${r.year}`)}</div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Source recovery provenance</h3><p class="aoAuditNote">Normalized Latin contributes reference structure only. Every inherited reference is resolved again in the requested language; literal Latin prose is never accepted as EN/FR completion.</p><div class="aoAuditMissing">${src.structuralExamples.slice(0,20).map(x=>`<article><b>${esc(x.date||'')} · ${esc((x.language||'').toUpperCase())} structural inheritance</b><small class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.path||'')}</small><div>${esc((x.sections||[]).join(', '))}</div></article>`).join('')||'<article>No structural inheritance was needed in this run.</article>'}${src.legacyCommonExamples.slice(0,20).map(x=>`<article><b>${esc(x.date||'')} · ${esc((x.language||'').toUpperCase())} historical Mass Common</b><small class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.path||'')}</small><div class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.source||'')}</div></article>`).join('')}</div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Calendar categories</h3><div class="aoAuditFieldGrid"><table class="aoAuditTable"><tr><th>Category</th><th>Days</th><th>Formularies</th><th>EN Proper</th><th>FR Proper</th><th>FR commentary</th></tr>${categoryRows(r)}</table></div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Scope boundary</h3><div class="aoAuditScope"><article><b>General Calendar</b><span>The date resolver certifies the formularies actually selected by the 1962 General Roman Calendar, including collisions, commemorations, inherited ferias and exceptional days.</span></article><article><b>Commons</b><span>The normalized 1962 layer contains ${r.supplementary?.commune?.normalized1962Files||28} Common paths. v14 recovers them through genuine same-language sources in this order: current Mass tree, pinned historical Mass Commune, then same-language horas Mass sections. Structural inheritance from normalized Latin carries references only, never Latin prose. Runtime field coverage remains the controlling test.</span></article><article><b>Votive Masses</b><span>v15 enumerates the pinned normalized Votive family separately from the date audit. Each formulary is resolved in LA/EN/FR with the same fail-closed rules; missing vernacular literal sections remain visible gaps.</span></article><article><b>Requiem</b><span>v15 explicitly audits the three All Souls Masses plus the daily Requiem. The daily Requiem may recover from the pinned historical C9 Mass-of-the-Dead corpus in the same language.</span></article></div></section>`}
function renderFields(r){return`<section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Proper fields</h3><p class="aoAuditNote">Counts are field occurrences on resolved dates; Latin presence defines whether a vernacular field is expected.</p><div class="aoAuditFieldGrid"><table class="aoAuditTable"><tr><th>Field</th><th>EN available</th><th>EN missing</th><th>FR available</th><th>FR missing</th></tr>${fieldRows(r)}</table></div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Exact Proper gaps</h3><div class="aoAuditMissing">${r.proper.missing.slice(0,250).map(x=>`<article data-severity="red"><b>${esc(x.date)} · ${esc(x.category)} · ${esc(x.lang.toUpperCase())}</b><small class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.sourcePath||x.properId)}</small><div>${esc((x.fields||[]).join(', '))}</div></article>`).join('')||'<article>No bilingual Proper-field gaps detected in this run.</article>'}</div></section>`}
function renderCommentary(r){return`<section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Commentary by Proper slot</h3><div class="aoAuditFieldGrid"><table class="aoAuditTable"><tr><th>Slot</th><th>Refs</th><th>EN integrated</th><th>FR integrated</th><th>FR link</th><th>FR missing</th></tr>${slotRows(r)}</table></div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Commentary gaps</h3><div class="aoAuditMissing">${r.commentary.items.filter(x=>x.en!=='full'||x.fr!=='integrated').slice(0,250).map(x=>`<article data-severity="${x.fr==='missing'||x.en==='missing'?'red':'amber'}"><b>${esc(x.reference||x.referenceFr||'Reference')}</b><small>${esc(FIELD_LABELS[x.slot]||x.slot)} · EN ${esc(x.en)} · FR ${esc(x.fr)}${x.frScope?' · '+esc(x.frScope):''}</small><div class="aoAuditMono">${esc((x.paths||[]).join(' · '))}</div></article>`).join('')||'<article>No integrated commentary gaps detected.</article>'}</div></section><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Unparsed / failed Scripture loads</h3><div class="aoAuditMissing">${r.commentary.failures.slice(0,200).map(x=>`<article data-severity="red"><b>${esc(FIELD_LABELS[x.slot]||x.slot)}</b><small>${esc((x.dates||[]).join(', '))}</small><div>${esc(x.error)}</div><div class="aoAuditMono">${esc((x.paths||[]).join(' · '))}</div></article>`).join('')||'<article>No Scripture parse/load failures recorded.</article>'}</div></section>`}
function renderExceptional(r){return`<section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Exceptional rites encountered</h3><div>${Object.entries(r.exceptional.profiles).map(([k,v])=>badge(`${k}: ${v}`)).join('')||'None'}</div><div class="aoAuditMissing" style="margin-top:10px">${r.exceptional.items.slice(0,160).map(x=>`<article><b>${esc(x.date)} · ${esc(x.profile)}</b><small class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.path)}</small><div>${esc(x.title)}</div></article>`).join('')||'<article>No exceptional rite profiles resolved.</article>'}</div></section>`}
function renderReport(r){lastReport=r;const o=document.getElementById('ao-content-audit-v14');if(!o)return;const box=o.querySelector('[data-auditresults]');const tabs=`<div class="aoAuditTabs">${[['summary','Summary'],['fields','Proper fields'],['commentary','Commentary'],['exceptional','Exceptional']].map(([id,l])=>`<button data-audittab="${id}" class="${activePanel===id?'active':''}">${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;const content=activePanel==='fields'?renderFields(r):activePanel==='commentary'?renderCommentary(r):activePanel==='exceptional'?renderExceptional(r):renderSummary(r);box.innerHTML=tabs+content;o.querySelectorAll('[data-audittab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{activePanel=b.dataset.audittab;renderReport(lastReport)})}
function downloadJSON(){if(!lastReport)return;const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(lastReport,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);const tail=lastReport.supplementaryOnly?'source-inventory':lastReport.matrix?lastReport.years.join('-'):lastReport.year;a.download=`ad-orientem-v18-${lastReport.supplementaryOnly?'supplementary':lastReport.matrix?'matrix':'calendar'}-${tail}.json`;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000)}
function downloadCSV(){if(!lastReport)return;if(lastReport.supplementaryOnly){const csv=supplementaryCSV(lastReport),blob=new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download='ad-orientem-v18-supplementary-gaps.csv';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);return}const rows=[['kind','date','category','language','slot_or_reference','path','detail']];for(const x of lastReport.proper.missing)rows.push(['proper',x.date,x.category,x.lang,(x.fields||[]).join(';'),x.sourcePath||x.properId||'','missing']);for(const x of lastReport.commentary.items.filter(y=>y.en!=='full'||y.fr!=='integrated'))rows.push(['commentary',(x.dates||[]).join(';'),(x.categories||[]).join(';'),'EN='+x.en+' FR='+x.fr,x.reference||x.referenceFr||'',(x.paths||[]).join(';'),x.frScope||'']);for(const x of lastReport.commentary.failures)rows.push(['scripture-failure',(x.dates||[]).join(';'),'','',x.slot,(x.paths||[]).join(';'),x.error]);for(const x of (lastReport.sources?.structuralExamples||[]))rows.push(['structural-inheritance',x.date||'','',''+(x.language||''),(x.sections||[]).join(';'),x.path||'','normalized Latin references only; no Latin prose']);for(const x of (lastReport.sources?.legacyCommonExamples||[]))rows.push(['legacy-mass-recovery',x.date||'','',''+(x.language||''),'',x.path||'',x.source||'']);const csv=rows.map(r=>r.map(v=>`"${String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',')).join('\n'),blob=new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download='ad-orientem-v18-gap-list.csv';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000)}
async function runSingle(year,verify){return auditYear(year,{verifyCommentary:verify,onProgress:progress})}
async function runMatrix(center,verify){const years=[center-2,center-1,center,center+1,center+2];const out=[];for(let i=0;i<years.length;i++){const y=years[i];progress(i/years.length,`Certification matrix · ${y} (${i+1}/${years.length})`);out.push(await auditYear(y,{verifyCommentary:verify,onProgress:(r,m)=>progress((i+r)/years.length,`${y} · ${m}`)}))}return mergeReports(out)}

function diagV15(){return{requestedFiles:[],cacheHits:[],referencesResolved:[],languageGaps:[],structuralInheritances:[],legacyCommonRecoveries:[],votiveRecoveries:[],warnings:[],errors:[]}}
function supplementTexts(p){const out=[];const add=(slot,v)=>{if(!v)return;if(Array.isArray(v)){v.forEach((x,i)=>add(`${slot}[${i+1}]`,x));return}if(typeof v==='object'){const lat=String(v.lat||'').trim();if(lat)out.push({slot,text:v})}};add('Introit',p.introit);add('Collect',p.collects);add('Epistle/Lesson',p.epistle);add('Gradual/Tract/Alleluia',p.gradual);add('Sequence',p.sequence);add('Gospel',p.gospel);add('Offertory',p.offertory);add('Secret',p.secrets);add('Communion',p.communion);add('Postcommunion',p.postcommunions);return out}
async function auditSupplementary(verifyCommentary=true){
 const rt=window.AO_RUNTIME_V8,pr=rt?.resolver?.properResolver;if(!pr)throw new Error('Proper resolver is not ready.');
 const report={version:'17',supplementaryOnly:true,generated:new Date().toISOString(),scope:'explicit non-calendar formularies',items:[],summary:{total:SUPPLEMENTARY_V15.length,resolved:0,unsupported:0,enComplete:0,frComplete:0,enExpected:0,enAvailable:0,frExpected:0,frAvailable:0},commentary:{attempted:0,enFull:0,frIntegrated:0,failures:[]},sourcePolicy:'same-language-only; normalized Latin may contribute references/Rule structure but never vernacular prose'};
 let i=0;
 for(const spec of SUPPLEMENTARY_V15){i++;progress(i/SUPPLEMENTARY_V15.length*.82,`Supplementary ${i}/${SUPPLEMENTARY_V15.length} · ${spec.label}`);if(!spec.path){report.summary.unsupported++;report.items.push({...spec,status:'unsupported',en:{expected:0,available:0,missing:['source formulary absent']},fr:{expected:0,available:0,missing:['source formulary absent']},diagnostic:{warnings:[spec.reason]}});continue}
  const d=diagV15();try{const meta={name:spec.label,nameFr:spec.label,rank:'supplementary',color:spec.family==='Requiem'?'black':'white',properId:`supplementary:${spec.id}`,path:spec.path,profile:spec.family==='Requiem'?'requiem_mass_1962':'ordinary_mass_1962',gloria:false,credo:false,inherited:false};const p=await pr.resolveProper(meta,d);report.summary.resolved++;const en=p.languageCoverage?.en||{expected:0,available:0,missing:[]},fr=p.languageCoverage?.fr||{expected:0,available:0,missing:[]};report.summary.enExpected+=en.expected||0;report.summary.enAvailable+=en.available||0;report.summary.frExpected+=fr.expected||0;report.summary.frAvailable+=fr.available||0;if(en.complete)report.summary.enComplete++;if(fr.complete)report.summary.frComplete++;const row={...spec,status:'resolved',sourcePath:p.sourcePath,en,fr,structuralInheritances:d.structuralInheritances||[],historicalCommonRecoveries:d.legacyCommonRecoveries||[],votiveRecoveries:d.votiveRecoveries||[],languageGaps:d.languageGaps||[],warnings:d.warnings||[],commentary:{references:0,enFull:0,frIntegrated:0,failures:[]}};
   if(verifyCommentary){for(const x of supplementTexts(p)){let doc;try{doc=await rt.scriptureService.load(x.slot==='Gospel'?'gospel':'scripture',x.text)}catch(e){row.commentary.failures.push({slot:x.slot,error:e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e)});continue}if(!doc?.reference&&!doc?.parts?.length)continue;const docs=doc.parts?.length?doc.parts:[doc];for(const part of docs){if(!part?.reference)continue;row.commentary.references++;report.commentary.attempted++;const enOk=part.reference.kind==='gospel'?!!part.englishCatena?.length:!!part.englishCommentary?.entries?.length||!!part.englishNotes?.length;const frOk=!!part.frenchCommentary?.entries?.length||!!part.frenchCatena?.length;if(enOk){row.commentary.enFull++;report.commentary.enFull++}if(frOk){row.commentary.frIntegrated++;report.commentary.frIntegrated++}}}report.commentary.failures.push(...row.commentary.failures.map(x=>({...x,id:spec.id,path:spec.path}))) }
   report.items.push(row)}catch(e){report.items.push({...spec,status:'error',error:e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e),en:{expected:0,available:0,missing:['resolution failed']},fr:{expected:0,available:0,missing:['resolution failed']},diagnostic:d});}
 }
 progress(.96,'Supplementary audit complete.');lastReport=report;return report
}
function suppPct(a,b){return b?Math.round(a/b*1000)/10:0}
function renderSupplementary(r){const o=document.getElementById('ao-content-audit-v14');if(!o)return;const box=o.querySelector('[data-auditresults]');const s=r.summary;const rows=r.items.map(x=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(x.label)}</b><small>${esc(x.family)}</small></td><td class="aoAuditMono">${esc(x.path||'—')}</td><td>${x.status==='unsupported'?badge('unsupported','bad'):badge(`${x.en.available}/${x.en.expected}`,x.en.complete?'ok':x.en.available?'warn':'bad')}<small>${esc((x.en.missing||[]).join(', '))}</small></td><td>${x.status==='unsupported'?badge('unsupported','bad'):badge(`${x.fr.available}/${x.fr.expected}`,x.fr.complete?'ok':x.fr.available?'warn':'bad')}<small>${esc((x.fr.missing||[]).join(', '))}</small></td><td>${x.commentary?`${x.commentary.frIntegrated}/${x.commentary.references}`:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('');box.innerHTML=`<div class="aoAuditSummary"><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${suppPct(s.enAvailable,s.enExpected)}%</b><span>supplementary EN fields</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${suppPct(s.frAvailable,s.frExpected)}%</b><span>supplementary FR fields</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${s.enComplete}/${s.resolved}</b><span>EN complete formularies</span></div><div class="aoAuditStat"><b>${s.frComplete}/${s.resolved}</b><span>FR complete formularies</span></div></div><section class="aoAuditSection"><h3>Supplementary formularies</h3><p class="aoAuditNote">These rows are not certified by a date traversal. Missing vernacular sections stay red; Latin content is never relabelled as EN or FR. Daily Requiem may recover from the pinned same-language historical C9 Mass corpus.</p><div class="aoAuditFieldGrid"><table class="aoAuditTable"><tr><th>Formulary</th><th>Source</th><th>EN</th><th>FR</th><th>FR commentary</th></tr>${rows}</table></div></section>`}
function supplementaryCSV(r){const rows=[['family','label','path','status','en_available','en_expected','en_missing','fr_available','fr_expected','fr_missing','commentary_fr','commentary_refs','detail']];for(const x of r.items)rows.push([x.family,x.label,x.path||'',x.status,x.en?.available||0,x.en?.expected||0,(x.en?.missing||[]).join(';'),x.fr?.available||0,x.fr?.expected||0,(x.fr?.missing||[]).join(';'),x.commentary?.frIntegrated||0,x.commentary?.references||0,x.reason||x.error||'']);return rows.map(row=>row.map(v=>`"${String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',')).join('\n')}

(()=>{'use strict';
window.AO_COMMENTARY_POLICY_V12={
 version:'12',
 routes:{
  en:{gospel:'Catena Aurea · Newman/Oxford',psalm:'St Robert Bellarmine · 1866; Haydock fallback',other:'Haydock Catholic Bible Commentary'},

 },
 rules:['same-language commentary only','no runtime machine translation','Péronne Wikisource chapter text is segmented only by explicit verse markers','Augustine/Raulx Psalm commentary is explicitly Psalm-level, not falsely verse-mapped','Glaire–Vigouroux notes are attached only to explicit verse-linked footnotes; chapter summaries enrich Context but do not count as verse commentary','external link does not count as integrated commentary','composite references are resolved passage-by-passage']

const GUIDE={
 en:{
  title:'How to use Ad Orientem at Mass',intro:'The app should help you follow the liturgy without turning the Mass into continuous screen-reading. Learn before or after Mass; during Mass, use the live guide as an orientation layer.',
  sections:[
   ['1 · Before Mass','Use Prepare for immediate preparation. Use Learn, Scripture Context and Commentary before Mass when you want to understand the day in depth. They are intentionally richer than the live screen.'],
   ['2 · Choose what you are following','“Follow what you hear” tracks the audible liturgy: the schola and everything the sacred ministers say or sing aloud. “Complete liturgical action” also follows silent and private priestly prayers and actions. The two modes answer different questions; neither changes the rite itself.'],
   ['3 · Read the cues','cues'],
   ['4 · Look up when the action matters','The screen is not meant to hold your eyes continuously. At elevations, the Gospel procession, incensations, Communion and other visually significant moments, LOOK cues deliberately direct attention back to the altar or ministers.'],
   ['5 · Expect simultaneous actions','At Sung and Solemn Mass the priest, ministers and schola often act at the same time. The choir may still be singing a Proper while the celebrant has moved ahead at the altar. Ad Orientem keeps those tracks distinct instead of forcing them into an artificial single sequence.'],
   ['6 · Low, Sung and Solemn Mass are not identical','Low Mass has no sung choir track. Missa cantata adds the sung Ordinary and Proper without the full deacon/subdeacon structure. Solemn Mass assigns proper actions to deacon, subdeacon and other ministers. The live guide changes its emphasis accordingly.'],
   ['7 · Rubric is not the same as local custom','Where congregational posture, responses or inherited practices vary legitimately, the app should label them as custom or participation guidance rather than presenting them as universal rubrics. The same applies to practices such as the second Confiteor before Communion.'],
   ['8 · Context and commentary are for understanding, not competing with worship','Open Reading when you need the appointed text, Context to see the surrounding chapter, and Commentary for historical Catholic exegesis. During Mass, use these only when genuinely helpful; the deeper reading is designed chiefly for before or after the liturgy.'],
   ['9 · After Mass','Use Thanksgiving after the final liturgical action rather than treating the Last Gospel or dismissal as the end of the app’s spiritual lifecycle. Prepare → Mass → Thanksgiving is the intended flow.']
  ],
  cues:{HEAR:'What is audibly being said or sung.',LOOK:'A moment when the visible liturgical action deserves your attention.',PRIEST:'What the celebrant is doing or praying, including actions not audible to the congregation.',YOU:'A response, posture or participation cue directed to the faithful; custom is labelled when it is not universal.'}
 },
 fr:{
  title:'Comment utiliser Ad Orientem à la Messe',intro:'L’application doit aider à suivre la liturgie sans transformer la Messe en lecture continue de l’écran. Apprenez avant ou après la Messe ; pendant la Messe, utilisez le guide en direct comme couche d’orientation.',
  sections:[
   ['1 · Avant la Messe','Utilisez Préparer pour la préparation immédiate. Utilisez Apprendre, le Contexte scripturaire et le Commentaire avant la Messe lorsque vous voulez comprendre le jour en profondeur. Ces modules sont volontairement plus riches que l’écran en direct.'],
   ['2 · Choisissez ce que vous suivez','« Suivre ce que vous entendez » suit la liturgie audible : la schola et tout ce que les ministres sacrés disent ou chantent à voix haute. « Action liturgique complète » suit aussi les prières et actions silencieuses ou privées du prêtre. Les deux modes répondent à des questions différentes ; aucun ne change le rite lui-même.'],
   ['3 · Lire les repères','cues'],
   ['4 · Levez les yeux quand l’action compte','L’écran n’est pas conçu pour retenir continuellement votre regard. Aux élévations, à la procession de l’Évangile, aux encensements, à la Communion et dans les autres moments visuellement importants, les repères LOOK ramènent délibérément l’attention vers l’autel ou les ministres.'],
   ['5 · Attendez-vous à des actions simultanées','À la Messe chantée et à la Messe solennelle, le prêtre, les ministres et la schola agissent souvent en même temps. Le chœur peut encore chanter un texte du Propre alors que le célébrant a déjà avancé à l’autel. Ad Orientem maintient ces lignes distinctes au lieu de les forcer dans une séquence artificiellement unique.'],
   ['6 · Messe basse, chantée et solennelle ne sont pas identiques','La Messe basse ne comporte pas de ligne chorale chantée. La Missa cantata ajoute l’Ordinaire et le Propre chantés sans toute la structure diacre/sous-diacre. La Messe solennelle attribue des actions propres au diacre, au sous-diacre et aux autres ministres. Le guide en direct adapte donc son accent.'],
   ['7 · Rubrique et coutume locale ne sont pas la même chose','Lorsque posture des fidèles, réponses ou pratiques héritées varient légitimement, l’application doit les identifier comme coutume ou conseil de participation au lieu de les présenter comme rubriques universelles. Il en va de même pour des usages comme le second Confiteor avant la Communion.'],
   ['8 · Contexte et commentaire servent à comprendre, non à concurrencer le culte','Ouvrez Lecture pour le texte prescrit, Contexte pour voir le chapitre environnant, et Commentaire pour l’exégèse catholique historique. Pendant la Messe, n’utilisez ces couches que lorsqu’elles aident réellement ; la lecture approfondie est conçue surtout pour avant ou après la liturgie.'],
   ['9 · Après la Messe','Utilisez l’Action de grâces après la dernière action liturgique au lieu de traiter le Dernier Évangile ou le renvoi comme la fin du cycle spirituel de l’application. Préparer → Messe → Action de grâces est le parcours voulu.']
  ],
  cues:{HEAR:'Ce qui est réellement dit ou chanté à voix audible.',LOOK:'Moment où l’action liturgique visible mérite votre attention.',PRIEST:'Ce que fait ou prie le célébrant, y compris ce qui n’est pas audible pour l’assemblée.',YOU:'Réponse, posture ou repère de participation adressé aux fidèles ; la coutume est signalée lorsqu’elle n’est pas universelle.'}
 }

function lang(){return document.querySelector('.aoLearnLang button.active')?.dataset.learnLang||document.querySelector('.homeScreen')?.dataset.homeLanguage||document.querySelector('.liveScreen')?.dataset.language||'en'}
function esc(v){return String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[c]))}
function openGuide(){document.getElementById('ao-use-guide-v9')?.remove();const L=GUIDE[lang()==='fr'?'fr':'en'];const root=document.createElement('div');root.id='ao-use-guide-v9';root.className='aoGuideBackdrop';root.innerHTML=`<section class="aoGuideSheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><header class="aoGuideTop"><div><small>Ad Orientem · guide</small><b>${esc(L.title)}</b></div><button data-guide-close aria-label="Close">×</button></header><div class="aoGuideHero"><small>${lang()==='fr'?'Pendant la Messe':'During Mass'}</small><h1>${esc(L.title)}</h1><p>${esc(L.intro)}</p></div>${L.sections.map(([h,p])=>`<section class="aoGuideSection"><h2>${esc(h)}</h2>${p==='cues'?`<div class="aoCueGrid">${Object.entries(L.cues).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="aoCue"><b>${esc(k)}</b><span>${esc(v)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`:`<p>${esc(p)}</p>`}</section>`).join('')}</section>`;document.body.appendChild(root);root.querySelector('[data-guide-close]').onclick=()=>root.remove();root.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===root)root.remove()})}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('[data-open-ao-use-guide]')){e.preventDefault();openGuide()}},true);
window.AO_USE_GUIDE_V9={open:openGuide,data:GUIDE};
})();
