<script id="ao-sequence-bridge-v23-js">
(function(){
'use strict';
const VERSION='23-live-sequence-certification';
const KEY='ao-active-resolved-mass-v23';
const OLD_KEYS=['ao-active-resolved-mass-v22','ao-active-resolved-mass-v21'];
function rt(){return window.AO_RUNTIME_V8||null}
function api(){return window.AO_CELEBRATION_API||null}
function arch(){return window.AO_CELEBRATION_ARCH_V1||null}
function state(){return rt()?.store?.getState?.()||null}
function fr(){return state()?.language==='fr'}
function L(en,frText){return fr()?frText:en}
function clone(v){return v==null?v:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}
function clearActive(){
  window.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION=null;
  try{localStorage.removeItem(KEY);OLD_KEYS.forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k))}catch{}
}
function saveActive(session){
  window.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION=session;
  try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(session));OLD_KEYS.forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k))}catch{}
}
function restoreActive(){
  try{
    let raw=localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if(!raw){for(const k of OLD_KEYS){raw=localStorage.getItem(k);if(raw)break}}
    if(!raw)return;
    const session=JSON.parse(raw),s=state();
    if(!session?.date||!session?.resolvedMass||!session?.proper)return clearActive();
    if(s?.selectedDate&&session.date!==s.selectedDate)return clearActive();
    window.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION=session;
  }catch{clearActive()}
}
function overrideProperFlags(proper,resolved){
  const out=clone(proper);
  if(typeof resolved.gloria==='boolean')out.hasGloria=out.showGloria=resolved.gloria;
  if(typeof resolved.credo==='boolean')out.hasCredo=out.showCredo=resolved.credo;
  if(resolved.celebrationType==='requiem'){
    out.isRequiem=true;
    out.riteProfile='requiem_1962';
  }
  return out;
}
function calendarProper(){const s=state();return s?.resolution?.proper?.status==='ready'?s.resolution.proper.data:null}
function firstCollect(proper){return (proper?.collects||[])[0]||proper?.collect||null}
function hasNuptialBlessings(proper){
  const ids=new Set((proper?.specialSections||[]).map(x=>x.id));
  return ['Benedictio Nuptialis 1','Benedictio Nuptialis 2','Benedictio Finalis'].every(id=>ids.has(id));
}
function assemblyPlanFor(decision,resolved,a){
  const code=String(decision?.code||'');
  if(/^votive-[12]-collect-retained$/.test(code))return {kind:'votive-collect-retained',retainedCollect:true,nuptialBlessings:false};
  if(code==='nuptial-mass-of-day')return {kind:'nuptial-mass-of-day',retainedCollect:true,nuptialBlessings:true};
  if(code==='requiem-all-souls-calendar')return {kind:'calendar-fallback',retainedCollect:false,nuptialBlessings:false};
  return null;
}
function assemblyStatus(){
  const a=arch(),A=api(),s=state();
  if(!a||!A||!s)return {ok:false,reason:L('Architecture is still starting.','L’architecture est encore en cours de démarrage.')};
  const decision=A.getRubricState?.().decision||A.resolveRubrics?.();
  const resolved=A.getResolvedMass?.();
  if(!decision||!resolved)return {ok:false,reason:L('Resolve the celebration first.','Résolvez d’abord la célébration.')};
  if(['impeded','prohibited'].includes(decision.status))return {ok:false,reason:decision.summary||L('This Mass cannot be started under the selected conditions.','Cette Messe ne peut pas être commencée dans les conditions sélectionnées.')};
  if(a.readiness?.loading)return {ok:false,reason:L('Resolving the formulary…','Résolution du formulaire…')};
  const cal=calendarProper();
  if(decision.status==='permitted'){
    if(resolved.celebrationId==='mass_of_day')return cal?{ok:true,resolved,proper:cal,calendar:true,assemblyPlan:null}:{ok:false,reason:L('The calendar Proper is not ready.','Le Propre du calendrier n’est pas prêt.')};
    if(!a.resolvedProper)return {ok:false,reason:L('Resolve the selected formulary before starting.','Résolvez le formulaire sélectionné avant de commencer.')};
    if(resolved.requestedCelebrationId==='nuptial'&&!hasNuptialBlessings(a.resolvedProper))return {ok:false,reason:L('The Nuptial formulary is missing one or more blessing sections.','Il manque une ou plusieurs sections de bénédiction dans le formulaire nuptial.')};
    return {ok:true,resolved,proper:a.resolvedProper,requestedProper:a.resolvedProper,calendar:false,assemblyPlan:null};
  }
  if(decision.status==='permittedWithChanges'){
    const plan=assemblyPlanFor(decision,resolved,a);
    if(!plan)return {ok:false,reason:L('This result still requires a local verification or an addition that is not sequence-certified.','Ce résultat exige encore une vérification locale ou un ajout qui n’est pas certifié dans la séquence.')};
    if(!cal)return {ok:false,reason:L('The calendar Proper is not ready.','Le Propre du calendrier n’est pas prêt.')};
    if(plan.kind==='calendar-fallback')return {ok:true,resolved,proper:cal,calendar:true,assemblyPlan:plan};
    const requested=a.resolvedProper;
    if(!requested)return {ok:false,reason:L('Resolve the requested formulary before assembling the Mass of the Day.','Résolvez le formulaire demandé avant d’assembler la Messe du jour.')};
    if(plan.retainedCollect&&!firstCollect(requested))return {ok:false,reason:L('The requested formulary does not contain the Collect required for this rubrical fallback.','Le formulaire demandé ne contient pas la Collecte requise pour ce cas rubrical.')};
    if(plan.nuptialBlessings&&!hasNuptialBlessings(requested))return {ok:false,reason:L('The Nuptial formulary is missing one or more blessing sections.','Il manque une ou plusieurs sections de bénédiction dans le formulaire nuptial.')};
    return {ok:true,resolved,proper:cal,requestedProper:requested,calendar:true,assemblyPlan:plan};
  }
  return {ok:false,reason:L('The rubrical result is not startable.','Le résultat rubrical ne permet pas de commencer la Messe.')};
}
async function startLive(){
  const a=arch(),A=api(),r=rt();
  if(!a||!A||!r?.store)return;
  if(a.actualCelebration?.id!=='mass_of_day'&&!a.resolvedProper&&!a.readiness?.loading){try{await A.resolveReadiness?.()}catch{}}
  const status=assemblyStatus();
  if(!status.ok){decorate();return}
  const resolved=clone(status.resolved);
  resolved.sourceDiagnostics={...(resolved.sourceDiagnostics||{}),sequenceCertification:'v23-matrix-16-of-16',sequenceContract:'ResolvedMass input only'};
  if(resolved.celebrationType==='requiem')resolved.exceptionalProfile='requiem-1962';
  const plan=status.assemblyPlan||null;
  const needsSession=!status.calendar||!!(plan&&(plan.retainedCollect||plan.nuptialBlessings));
  if(!needsSession){
    clearActive();
  }else{
    const proper=overrideProperFlags(status.proper,resolved);
    const session={
      version:VERSION,
      date:safeDate(),
      startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
      requestedType:a.actualCelebration?.type||resolved.celebrationType,
      requestedTitle:(a.actualCelebration?.id&&window.AO_CELEBRATION_CATALOGUE?.[a.actualCelebration.id]?.title)||null,
      resolvedMass:resolved,
      proper,
      requestedProper:clone(status.requestedProper||a.resolvedProper),
      assemblyPlan:clone(plan),
      themePlan:{primary:resolved.colour||proper.color||'Green',massColor:resolved.colour||proper.color||'Green'}
    };
    saveActive(session);
  }
  r.store.dispatch({type:'live-form-session',form:a.celebrationForm||'sung'});
  r.store.dispatch({type:'live-follow-mode-session',mode:a.followMode||'vox'});
  a.stage=null;a.history=[];
  document.getElementById('ao-mass-flow-v1')?.remove();
  r.store.dispatch({type:'enter-live'});
}
function safeDate(){return state()?.selectedDate||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}
function setTextIfChanged(el,value){if(!el)return false;const next=String(value??'');if(el.textContent===next)return false;el.textContent=next;return true}
function decorate(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('ao-mass-flow-v1');if(!wrap)return;
  const buttons=[...wrap.querySelectorAll('.aoFlowActions .primary')];
  const target=buttons.find(b=>/Live sequence wiring|Raccordement à la séquence|Start Mass|Commencer la Messe/i.test(b.textContent||'')||b.dataset.aoStartLive!==undefined);
  if(!target)return;
  const st=assemblyStatus();
  setTextIfChanged(target,st.ok?L('Start Mass →','Commencer la Messe →'):st.reason);
  target.disabled=!st.ok;
  if(st.ok)target.dataset.aoStartLive='';else delete target.dataset.aoStartLive;
  let note=target.parentElement?.nextElementSibling;
  if(!note||!note.classList?.contains('aoBridgeNote')){note=document.createElement('div');note.className='aoBridgeNote';target.parentElement?.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',note)}
  note.classList.toggle('ok',!!st.ok);
  let noteText=st.reason;
  if(st.ok){
    const kind=st.assemblyPlan?.kind;
    noteText=kind==='nuptial-mass-of-day'
      ? L('Mass of the Day assembled with the Nuptial Collect and the Nuptial Blessing in their prescribed places.','Messe du jour assemblée avec la Collecte nuptiale et la Bénédiction nuptiale à leurs places prescrites.')
      : kind==='votive-collect-retained'
      ? L('Mass of the Day assembled with the retained Collect of the impeded votive Mass under one conclusion.','Messe du jour assemblée avec la Collecte conservée de la Messe votive empêchée sous une seule conclusion.')
      : kind==='calendar-fallback'
      ? L('The rubrical result resolves to the calendar Mass; the normal live sequence will be used.','Le résultat rubrical renvoie à la Messe du calendrier ; la séquence normale sera utilisée.')
      : L('ResolvedMass is ready. The live engine will render this already-resolved celebration; it will not recalculate rubrical permission.','ResolvedMass est prêt. Le moteur en direct rendra cette célébration déjà résolue ; il ne recalculera pas la permission rubricale.');
  }
  setTextIfChanged(note,noteText);
}
function install(){
  restoreActive();
  const r=rt();if(!r?.store){setTimeout(install,80);return}
  let lastDate=r.store.getState().selectedDate;
  r.store.subscribe(s=>{if(s.selectedDate!==lastDate){lastDate=s.selectedDate;clearActive()}queueMicrotask(decorate)});
  decorate();
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const prepareNext=e.target?.closest?.('[data-prepare-next]');
  if(prepareNext&&state()?.route==='prepare'){
    const label=(prepareNext.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    if(/Follow Mass|Follow Liturgy|Suivre la Messe|Suivre la liturgie/i.test(label)&&api()?.openPreflight){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();rt()?.store?.dispatch?.({type:'leave-prepare'});queueMicrotask(()=>api()?.openPreflight?.());return}
  }
  const b=e.target?.closest?.('[data-ao-start-live]');if(!b)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();void startLive();
},true);
window.AO_SEQUENCE_BRIDGE_V23={version:VERSION,startLive,getAssemblyStatus:assemblyStatus,clearActive,decorate,getActive:()=>window.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION||null};window.AO_SEQUENCE_BRIDGE_V22=window.AO_SEQUENCE_BRIDGE_V23;
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',install,{once:true});else install();
})();
</script>

French follows the traditional parallel text published by Introibo.","frenchWitnessUrl":"https://introibo.fr/13-06-St-Antoine-de-Padoue"},"sourceAuditVersion":"41.4"},"sacred_heart_short_prayer":{"id":"sacred_heart_short_prayer","title":"Act of Reparation to the Sacred Heart of Jesus","titleFr":"Acte de réparation au Sacré-Cœur de Jésus","en":"Most Sweet Jesus, whose overflowing charity for men is requited by so much forgetfulness, negligence and contempt, behold us prostrate before Thee, eager to repair by a special act of homage the cruel indifference and injuries to which Thy loving Heart is everywhere subjected.\n\nMindful, alas, that we ourselves have had a share in such great indignities, which we now deplore from the depths of our hearts, we humbly ask Thy pardon and declare our readiness to atone by voluntary expiation, not only for our own personal offenses, but also for the sins of those who, straying far from the path of salvation, refuse in their obstinate infidelity to follow Thee, their Shepherd and Leader, or, renouncing the promises of their baptism, have cast off the sweet yoke of Thy Law.\n\nWe are now resolved to expiate each and every deplorable outrage committed against Thee; we are now determined to make amends for the manifold offenses against Christian modesty in unbecoming dress and behavior, for all the foul seductions laid to ensnare the feet of the innocent, for the frequent violations of Sundays and holydays, and the shocking blasphemies uttered against Thee and Thy Saints. We wish also to make amends for the insults to which Thy Vicar on earth and Thy priests are subjected, for the profanation, by conscious neglect or terrible acts of sacrilege, of the very Sacrament of Thy Divine Love, and lastly for the public crimes of nations who resist the rights and teaching authority of the Church which Thou hast founded.\n\nWould that we were able to wash away such abominations with our blood.\n\nWe now offer, in reparation for these violations of Thy Divine Honor, the satisfaction Thou once made to Thy Eternal Father on the Cross and which Thou continuest to renew daily on our altars; we offer it in union with the acts of atonement of Thy Virgin Mother and all the Saints and of the pious faithful on earth; and we sincerely promise to make recompense, as far as we can with the help of Thy grace, for all neglect of Thy great love and for the sins we and others have committed in the past. Henceforth, we will live a life of unswerving faith, of purity of conduct, of perfect observance of the precepts of the Gospel and especially that of charity. We promise to the best of our power to prevent others from offending Thee and to bring as many as possible to follow Thee.\n\nO loving Jesus, through the intercession of the Blessed Virgin Mother, our model in reparation, deign to receive the voluntary offering we make of this act of expiation; and by the crowning gift of perseverance keep us faithful unto death in our duty and the allegiance we owe to Thee, so that we may all one day come to that happy home, where with the Father and the Holy Spirit Thou livest and reignest, God, forever and ever. Amen.","la":"Iesu dulcissime, cuius effusa in homines caritas, tanta oblivione, neglegentia, contemptione, ingratissime rependitur, en nos, ante conspectum tuum provoluti, tam nefariam hominum socordiam iniuriasque, quibus undique amantissimum Cor tuum afficitur, peculiari honore resarcire contendimus.\n\nAttamen, memores tantae nos quoque indignitatis non expertes aliquando fuisse, indeque vehementissimo dolore commoti, tuam in primis misericordiam nobis imploramus, parati, voluntaria expiatione compensare flagitia non modo quae ipsi patravimus, sed etiam illorum, qui, longe a salutis via aberrantes vel te pastorem ducemque sectari detrectant, in sua infidelitate obstinati, vel, baptismatis promissa conculcantes, suavissimum tuae legis iugum excusserunt.\n\nQuae deploranda crimina, cum universa expiare contendimus, tum nobis singula resarcienda proponimus: vitae cultusque immodestiam atque turpitudines, tot corruptelae pedicas innocentium animis instructas, dies festos violatos, exsecranda in te tuosque Sanctos iactata maledicta atque in tuum Vicarium ordinemque sacerdotalem convicia irrogata, ipsum denique amoris divini Sacramentum vel neglectum vel horrendis sacrilegiis profanatum, publica postremo nationum delicta, quae Ecclesiae a te institutae iuribus magisterioque reluctantur.\n\nQuae utinam crimina sanguine ipsi nostro eluere possemus! Interea ad violatum divinum honorem resarciendum, quam Tu olim Patri in Cruce satisfactionem obtulisti quamque cotidie in altaribus renovare pergis, hanc eamdem nos tibi praestamus, cum Virginis Matris, omnium Sanctorum, piorum quoque fidelium expiationibus coniunctam, ex animo spondentes, cum praeterita nostra aliorumque peccata ac tanti amoris incuriam firma fide, candidis vitae moribus, perfecta legis evangelicae, caritatis potissimum, observantia, quantum in nobis erit, gratia tua favente, nos esse compensaturos, tum iniurias tibi inferendas pro viribus prohibituros, et quam plurimos potuerimus ad tui sequelam convocaturos.\n\nExcipias, quaesumus, benignissime Iesu, beata Virgine Maria Reparatrice intercedente, voluntarium huius expiationis obsequium nosque in officio tuique servitio fidissimos ad mortem usque velis, magno illo perseverantiae munere, continere, ut ad illam tandem patriam perveniamus omnes, ubi Tu cum Patre et Spiritu Sancto vivis et regnas in saecula saeculorum. Amen.","fr":"Très doux Jésus, dont l’immense amour pour les hommes a été avec tant d’ingratitude payé par l’oubli, la négligence, le mépris, voici que, prosternés devant vos autels, nous voulons réparer par des témoignages particuliers d’honneur une froideur si indigne et les injures qui blessent de toutes parts votre Cœur très aimant.\n\nNous souvenant toutefois que nous n’avons pas toujours été, nous non plus, exempts de cette indignité et en ressentant une très profonde douleur, nous implorons tout d’abord sur nous votre miséricorde, disposés à réparer par une expiation volontaire non seulement les péchés que nous avons commis nous-mêmes, mais encore les fautes de ceux qui, égarés loin de la voie du salut, refusent de vous suivre comme leur pasteur et leur guide, s’obstinant dans leur infidélité, ou qui, reniant les promesses de leur baptême, ont secoué le joug très suave de votre loi.\n\nCes crimes déplorables que nous voulons expier tous, nous nous proposons aussi de les réparer chacun en particulier : l’immodestie et les hontes de la conduite et des vêtements ; les embûches tendues par la corruption aux âmes innocentes, la violation des fêtes, les outrages odieux lancés contre vous et vos saints, ainsi que les insultes adressées à votre Vicaire et à l’ordre sacerdotal, la négligence à l’égard du Sacrement du divin amour ou sa profanation par d’horribles sacrilèges, enfin les crimes publics des nations qui combattent les droits et le magistère de l’Église que vous avez instituée.\n\nQue ne nous est-il donné de pouvoir laver ces crimes de notre sang ! Pour réparer l’honneur divin outragé, nous vous présentons, unie aux expiations de la Vierge Marie, de tous les saints et des fidèles pieux, l’expiation que vous avez un jour offerte au Père sur la croix et que vous continuez de renouveler chaque jour sur les autels ; nous vous promettons de tout notre Cœur de réparer pour autant qu’il sera en nous et avec le secours de votre grâce nos fautes passées comme celles des autres et une si grande négligence de votre amour par une foi inébranlable, par une vie pure, par l’observation parfaite de la loi évangélique, en particulier de la charité, d’empêcher selon nos forces les offenses qui s’adresseraient à vous et d’amener le plus d’hommes possible à votre suite.\n\nTrès bon Jésus, recevez, nous vous en prions, par l’intercession de la bienheureuse Vierge Marie Réparatrice, l’hommage volontaire de notre expiation et daignez, par le don précieux de la persévérance, nous garder fidèles jusqu’à la mort dans votre obéissance et votre service, afin que nous parvenions enfin tous à cette patrie où vous vivez et régnez, vrai Dieu, avec le Père et l’Esprit-Saint, dans les siècles des siècles. const root=document.createElement('div');root.id='aoPrayerBookRoot';root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.appendChild(root);

let externalReturn=null;
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
function lang(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ao2:settings:v1')||'{}');return x.language==='fr'?'fr':'en'}catch{return 'en'}}
const t=(en,fr)=>lang()==='fr'?fr:en;
function top(title,sub=t('Traditional prayer book','Livre de prières traditionnel')){return `<header class="pbTop aoHasModuleHome"><button data-pb-back aria-label="${t('Back','Retour')}">←</button><div><small>${esc(sub)}</small><h1>${esc(title)}</h1></div><button type="button" class="aoModuleHome" data-app-home aria-label="${t('Return to Home','Retour à l’accueil')}">⌂</button><span class="pbLangBadge">${lang().toUpperCase()}</span></header>`}
function source(p){const raw=String(p?.source||'').trim(),internal=/lab-ora/i.test(raw),label=internal?'':raw,note=String(p?.note||'').trim(),url=internal?'':String(p?.sourceUrl||'').trim();if(!label&&!note&&!url)return'';return `<details class="aoSourceDisclosure pbSourceCompact"><summary>${label?`${t('Source','Source')} · ${esc(label)}`:t('Source','Source')}</summary><div class="aoSourceDetail">${note?`<p>${esc(note)}</p>`:''}<div class="aoSourceActions">${url?`<a href="${esc(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${t('View source','Voir la source')} ↗</a>`:''}<button type="button" data-v25-destination="sources">${t('Sources & editions','Sources & éditions')}</button></div></div></details>`}
function flipText(la,vern,id='main',face=null){const v=vern||'';if(!la)return `<div class="pbPrayerText" style="cursor:default">${esc(v)}</div>`;return `<button class="pbPrayerText" data-pb-flip="${esc(id)}" data-face="latin"><span data-pb-latin>${esc(la)}</span><span data-pb-vern hidden>${esc(v)}</span></button><div class="pbFlipHint" data-pb-flip-hint>${t('Tap the text · show English','Touchez le texte · afficher le français')}</div>`}
function prayerTitle(p){return lang()==='fr'?(p.titleFr||p.title):p.title}
function open(opts={}){externalReturn=opts.returnContext||null;S.view='hub';S.prayer=null;render();root.classList.add('open');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');document.body.style.overflow='hidden'}
function close(opts={}){root.classList.remove('open');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.style.overflow='';S.view='hub';const ret=externalReturn;externalReturn=null;if(!opts.silent&&ret)queueMicrotask(()=>window.AO_NAV_V362?.restore?.(ret))}
function go(v){S.view=v;render();root.scrollTo(0,0)}
function back(){if(externalReturn)return close();if(S.view==='hub')return close();if(S.view==='prayer')return go('hub');if(['confession','rosary','angelus','stations','benediction'].includes(S.view))return go('hub');go('hub')}
function openModule(id,opts={}){open(opts);if(id==='confession')S.confStep=0;if(id==='rosary'){S.rosarySet=null;S.rosaryStep=0}if(id==='stations')S.station=0;if(id==='benediction')S.benedictionStep=0;if(['confession','rosary','angelus','stations','benediction'].includes(id)){go(id);return true}return false}
function openPrayer(id,opts={}){if(!D.prayers[id])return false;open(opts);S.prayer=id;go('prayer');return true}
function renderHub(){const q=S.query.trim().toLowerCase();const cats=Object.entries(D.categories).map(([id,n])=>{let ps=Object.values(D.prayers).filter(p=>p.category===id);if(q)ps=ps.filter(p=>[p.title,p.titleFr,p.en,p.fr,p.la].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q));if(!ps.length)return'';return `<section class="pbSection"><div class="pbSectionHead"><h2>${esc(n[lang()]||n.en)}</h2><small>${ps.length}</small></div><div class="pbPrayerGrid">${ps.map(p=>`<button class="pbPrayerCard" data-pb-prayer="${esc(p.id)}"><strong>${esc(prayerTitle(p))}</strong><span>${p.la?t('Latin first · tap for English','Latin d’abord · toucher pour le français'):t('Vernacular text','Texte vernaculaire')}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></section>`}).join('');
return `<div class="pbShell">${top(t('Prayer Book','Livre de prières'))}<section class="pbHero"><div class="eyebrow">AD ORIENTEM · TLM</div><h2>${t('Pray the tradition around the Mass','Prier dans la tradition autour de la Messe')}</h2><p>${t('Confession, Rosary, Angelus, the XIV Stations and a curated treasury of prayers commonly used in traditional Latin-Rite life. Latin prayer texts flip in place to the current vernacular when tapped.','Confession, Rosaire, Angélus, XIV Stations et trésor de prières couramment utilisées dans la vie traditionnelle du rite latin. Later additions are included only when identified as such; for example, the Luminous Mysteries are marked as the optional 2002 addition, and the current Litany of Loreto is kept distinct from the 1962-era form.','Règle éditoriale : cette bibliothèque privilégie le corpus traditionnel. function renderPrayer(){const p=D.prayers[S.prayer];if(!p)return go('hub');return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbReader">${top(prayerTitle(p))}<div class="pbMeta"><span class="pbTag">${p.la?'LAT → '+lang().toUpperCase():lang().toUpperCase()}</span><span class="pbTag">${esc(D.categories[p.category]?.[lang()]||'')}</span></div>${flipText(p.la,p[lang()]||p.en)}${source(p)}</div></div>`}
function currentDate(){return new Date()}
function easter(y){const a=y%19,b=Math.floor(y/100),c=y%100,d=Math.floor(b/4),e=b%4,f=Math.floor((b+8)/25),g=Math.floor((b-f+1)/3),h=(19*a+b-d-g+15)%30,i=Math.floor(c/4),k=c%4,l=(32+2*e+2*i-h-k)%7,m=Math.floor((a+11*h+22*l)/451),month=Math.floor((h+l-7*m+114)/31)-1,day=(h+l-7*m+114)%31+1;return new Date(y,month,day)}
function paschal(){const d=currentDate(),e=easter(d.getFullYear()),p=new Date(e);p.setDate(p.getDate()+49);return d>=e&&d<=p}
function suggestedMystery(){const d=currentDate(),dow=d.getDay();if(dow===1||dow===4)return'joyful';if(dow===2||dow===5)return'sorrowful';if(dow===3||dow===6)return'glorious';const e=easter(d.getFullYear()),ash=new Date(e);ash.setDate(ash.getDate()-46);const adv=new Date(d.getFullYear(),11,25);while(adv.getDay()!==0)adv.setDate(adv.getDate()-1);adv.setDate(adv.getDate()-21);if(d>=ash&&d<e)return'sorrowful';if(d>=adv||d<new Date(d.getFullYear(),0,7))return'joyful';return'glorious'}
function renderAngelus(){const useRegina=S.angelusOverride?S.angelusOverride==='regina':paschal(),x=useRegina?D.regina:D.angelus;const appendix=!useRegina&&S.angelusConclusion?`<div class="pbMiniPrayer"><div class="pbKicker">${t('Optional traditional conclusion','Conclusion traditionnelle facultative')}</div>${flipText(D.angelusAppendix.la,D.angelusAppendix[lang()],'angelus-appendix',false)}</div>`:'';return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbReader">${top(useRegina?'Regina Cæli':'Angelus')}<div class="pbContextNotice">${useRegina?t('Paschaltide: Regina Cæli is shown in place of the Angelus. It is customarily prayed standing.','Temps pascal : le Regina Cæli est proposé à la place de l’Angélus. Il se prie traditionnellement debout.'):t('Traditional practice: morning, noon and evening. Older prayer books commonly direct kneeling, with Sunday and Paschal exceptions; legitimate local custom prevails.','Pratique traditionnelle : matin, midi et soir. Les anciens livres de prières indiquent souvent de s’agenouiller, avec des exceptions le dimanche et au temps pascal ; suivez l’usage local légitime.')}</div>${flipText(x.la,x[lang()],'angelus-main',false)}${!useRegina?`<label class="pbContextNotice" style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center"><input type="checkbox" data-pb-angelus-conclusion ${S.angelusConclusion?'checked':''}> <span>${t('Add the traditional conclusion: three Gloria Patri, prayer for the faithful departed, and Divinum auxilium','Ajouter la conclusion traditionnelle : trois Gloria Patri, prière pour les fidèles défunts et Divinum auxilium')}</span></label>${appendix}`:''}<div class="pbFlowNav"><button data-pb-toggle-angelus>${useRegina?t('Show Angelus instead','Afficher plutôt l’Angélus'):t('Show Regina Cæli instead','Afficher plutôt le Regina Cæli')}</button></div><div class="pbSource"><strong>${t('Tradition / current clarification','Tradition / précision actuelle')}</strong><br>${t('The Angelus and Regina Cæli are devotions rather than parts of the Mass. Traditional sources document the posture customs; the three Gloria Patri are presented as an optional historical conclusion, not a condition of the prayer.','L’Angélus et le Regina Cæli sont des dévotions et non des parties de la Messe. Les sources traditionnelles documentent les usages de posture ; les trois Gloria Patri sont présentés comme une conclusion historique facultative, non comme une condition de la prière.')}</div></div></div>`}
function renderConfession(){const l=lang(),exam=l==='fr'?D.examFr:D.exam;const steps=l==='fr'?[['Réception','Suivez le prêtre. Faites le signe de la Croix selon l’usage.'],['Commencez simplement','« Mon Père, bénissez-moi parce que j’ai péché. Je ne me suis pas confessé depuis… » est une formule traditionnelle courante, non un script obligatoire.'],['Confessez vos péchés','Confessez d’abord les péchés mortels dont vous vous souvenez, selon leur espèce et leur nombre approximatif autant que possible ; puis les autres péchés que vous désirez accuser.'],['Conseil et pénitence','Écoutez le conseil du prêtre et acceptez la satisfaction ou pénitence.'],['Acte du pénitent','Exprimez votre contrition lorsque le prêtre vous y invite.'],['Absolution','Le prêtre seul donne l’absolution sacramentelle. Écoutez et répondez Amen.'],['Renvoi','Recevez le sacrement comme achevé ; ne recommencez pas immédiatement votre examen en sortant.'],['Après','Remerciez Dieu, accomplissez rapidement la pénitence et réparez le tort concret lorsque la justice l’exige.']]:[['Reception','Follow the priest. Make the Sign of the Cross as directed or customary.'],['Begin simply','“Bless me, Father, for I have sinned. My last Confession was … ago.” is a common traditional opening, not a mandatory script.'],['Confess your sins','Confess remembered mortal sins first, by kind and approximate number as best you can; then other sins you wish to bring.'],['Counsel and penance','Listen to the priest’s counsel and accept the satisfaction or penance given.'],['Prayer of the penitent','Express contrition when invited.'],['Absolution','The priest alone gives sacramental absolution. Listen attentively and answer Amen.'],['Dismissal','Receive the sacrament as complete; do not restart the examination as you leave.'],['Afterwards','Thank God, perform the assigned penance promptly, and make concrete repair where justice requires it.']];const st=steps[S.confStep];const act=D.prayers.sacrament_act_of_contrition;return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Confession','Confession'))}<section class="pbHero"><div class="eyebrow">${t('Prepare · confess · give thanks','Préparer · confesser · rendre grâce')}</div><h2>${t('A practical companion, not a substitute for the priest','Un compagnon pratique, non un substitut au prêtre')}</h2><p>${t('Current sacramental law and the traditional devotional manner are kept distinct. The app does not simulate absolution.','La discipline sacramentelle actuelle et la manière dévotionnelle traditionnelle restent distinctes. L’application ne simule jamais l’absolution.')}</p></section><details class="pbInfoDetails" open><summary>${t('Examination of conscience','Examen de conscience')}</summary><p class="pbLead">${esc(exam.intro)}</p><div class="pbExam">${exam.sections.map(s=>`<details><summary>${esc(s[0])}</summary><ul>${s[1].map(q=>`<li>${esc(q)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></details>`).join('')}</div></details><div class="pbProgress"><span style="width:${((S.confStep+1)/steps.length*100).toFixed(1)}%"></span></div><section class="pbFlowCard"><div class="pbKicker">${S.confStep+1} / ${steps.length}</div><h2>${esc(st[0])}</h2><p>${esc(st[1])}</p>${S.confStep===4?`<div class="pbMiniPrayer">${flipText(act.la,act[l]||act.en,'conf-act')}</div>`:''}</section><nav class="pbFlowNav"><button data-pb-conf-prev ${S.confStep===0?'disabled':''}>← ${t('Previous','Précédent')}</button><button data-pb-conf-next>${S.confStep===steps.length-1?t('Finish','Terminer'):t('Next','Suivant')} →</button></nav><div class="pbSource"><strong>${t('Current Church discipline','Discipline actuelle de l’Église')}</strong><br>${t('Catechism §§1450–1460; CIC canons 987–988. function rosaryNames(){return lang()==='fr'?{joyful:['L’Annonciation','La Visitation','La Nativité','La Présentation','Le Recouvrement de Jésus au Temple'],sorrowful:['L’Agonie au jardin','La Flagellation','Le Couronnement d’épines','Le Portement de la Croix','La Crucifixion'],glorious:['La Résurrection','L’Ascension','La Descente du Saint-Esprit','L’Assomption','Le Couronnement de Marie'],luminous:['Le Baptême du Seigneur','Les Noces de Cana','L’Annonce du Royaume','La Transfiguration','L’Institution de l’Eucharistie']}:{joyful:D.mysteries.joyful,sorrowful:D.mysteries.sorrowful,glorious:D.mysteries.glorious,luminous:D.mysteries.luminous}}
function prayerBundle(keys){const l=lang(),rp=D.rosaryPrayers,parts=keys.map(k=>({la:rp.la[k][1],v:rp[l][k][1],label:rp[l][k][0]}));return parts.map((p,i)=>`<div class="pbMiniPrayer"><div class="pbKicker">${esc(p.label)}</div>${flipText(p.la,p.v,'rb'+i,false)}</div>`).join('')}
function renderRosary(){if(!S.rosarySet){const sug=suggestedMystery();return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Holy Rosary','Saint Rosaire'))}<section class="pbHero"><div class="eyebrow">${t('Traditional cycle','Cycle traditionnel')}</div><h2>${t('Choose the mysteries','Choisissez les mystères')}</h2><p>${t('The three traditional sets are primary. const set=S.rosarySet,names=rosaryNames()[set],total=7,i=S.rosaryStep;let body='';if(i===0)body=`<div class="pbKicker">${t('Opening','Ouverture')}</div><h2>${t('Begin the Rosary','Commencez le Rosaire')}</h2>${prayerBundle(['sign','credo','pater'])}<div class="pbContextNotice">${t('Then: three Hail Marys and one Glory Be.','Puis : trois Ave Maria et un Gloria Patri.')}</div>${prayerBundle(['ave','gloria'])}`;else if(i<=5){const n=i-1;body=`<div class="pbKicker">${t('Mystery','Mystère')} ${i} / 5</div><div class="pbMysteryTitle">${esc(names[n])}</div><p>${t('Announce the mystery; pause briefly to contemplate it. Then pray the decade.','Annoncez le mystère ; faites une brève pause pour le contempler. Puis priez la dizaine.')}</p>${prayerBundle(['pater'])}<div class="pbCounter">${Array.from({length:10},()=>'<i></i>').join('')}</div>${prayerBundle(['ave','gloria','fatima'])}`;}else body=`<div class="pbKicker">${t('Conclusion','Conclusion')}</div><h2>${t('Conclude the Rosary','Concluez le Rosaire')}</h2><div class="pbMiniPrayer">${flipText(D.salve.la,D.salve[lang()],'salve',false)}</div><div class="pbMiniPrayer">${flipText(D.rosaryCollect.la,D.rosaryCollect[lang()],'collect',false)}</div>`;return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Holy Rosary','Saint Rosaire'))}<div class="pbProgress"><span style="width:${((i+1)/total*100).toFixed(1)}%"></span></div><section class="pbFlowCard">${body}</section><nav class="pbFlowNav"><button data-pb-rosary-prev ${i===0?'disabled':''}>← ${t('Previous','Précédent')}</button><button data-pb-rosary-next>${i===total-1?t('Finish','Terminer'):t('Next','Suivant')} →</button></nav><button class="pbSecondary" data-pb-rosary-change style="margin-top:10px">${t('Change mysteries','Changer de mystères')}</button></div></div>`}
function renderStations(){const i=S.station,l=lang(),titles=D.stationTitles[l]||D.stationTitles.en;if(i===0)return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Stations of the Cross','Chemin de Croix'))}<section class="pbHero"><div class="eyebrow">VIA CRUCIS · XIV</div><h2>${t('St Alphonsus Liguori','Saint Alphonse de Liguori')}</h2><p>${t('Pray the traditional fourteen Stations in the St Alphonsus form. Guided includes the meditation and devotional prayer; Simple keeps the station, versicle/response and ordinary station prayers.','Priez les quatorze Stations traditionnelles selon la forme de saint Alphonse. Le mode guidé ajoute la méditation et la prière dévotionnelle ; le mode simple conserve la station, le verset/répons et les prières ordinaires.')}</p></section><div class="pbModuleGrid"><button class="pbModule ${S.stationMode==='guided'?'featured':''}" data-pb-station-mode="guided"><strong>${t('Guided','Guidé')}</strong><span>${t('St Alphonsus meditation + prayer','Méditation + prière de saint Alphonse')}</span></button><button class="pbModule ${S.stationMode==='simple'?'featured':''}" data-pb-station-mode="simple"><strong>${t('Simple','Simple')}</strong><span>Pater · Ave · Gloria</span></button></div><button class="pbPrimary" data-pb-station-next>${t('Begin the XIV Stations','Commencer les XIV Stations')} →</button><div class="pbSource"><strong>${t('Sources','Sources')}</strong><br>St Alphonsus Maria de Liguori · <em>Way of the Cross</em> (1787). ${t('English devotional form based on the 1888 Baltimore Manual witness; the French rendering is retained as a separately source-audited traditional-register translation.','Forme dévotionnelle anglaise fondée sur le témoin du Manual de Baltimore de 1888 ; la version française est conservée comme traduction en registre traditionnel auditée séparément.')}</div></div></div>`;const n=i-1,a=D.alphonsus[l]||D.alphonsus.en,vr=D.stationVR,ala=D.alphonsus.la;const guided=S.stationMode==='guided'?`<h3>${t('Consider','Considérez')}</h3><p>${esc(a[n].consider)}</p><h3>${t('Prayer of St Alphonsus','Prière de saint Alphonse')}</h3>${flipText(ala[n].prayer,a[n].prayer,'station-alphonsus-'+n,false)}`:'';return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Stations of the Cross','Chemin de Croix'))}<div class="pbProgress"><span style="width:${(i/14*100).toFixed(1)}%"></span></div><section class="pbFlowCard"><div class="pbKicker">${['I','II','III','IV','V','VI','VII','VIII','IX','X','XI','XII','XIII','XIV'][n]} · XIV</div><h2>${esc(titles[n])}</h2><div class="pbMiniPrayer">${flipText(vr.la.join('\n'),vr[l].join('\n'),'station-vr-'+n,false)}</div>${guided}<div class="pbContextNotice">${t('Traditional station prayers: Pater noster · Ave Maria · Gloria Patri.','Prières traditionnelles de la station : Pater noster · Ave Maria · Gloria Patri.')}</div>${prayerBundle(['pater','ave','gloria'])}</section><nav class="pbFlowNav"><button data-pb-station-prev>← ${t('Previous','Précédent')}</button><button data-pb-station-next>${i===14?t('Finish','Terminer'):t('Next station','Station suivante')} →</button></nav></div></div>`}
function renderBenediction(){const seq=['benediction_o_salutaris','silence','benediction_tantum_ergo','benediction_versicles','benediction_divine_praises','benediction_adoremus_ps116'],i=S.benedictionStep,id=seq[i],p=D.prayers[id];let body='';if(id==='silence')body=`<div class="pbKicker">${t('Adoration','Adoration')}</div><h2>${t('Remain before the Blessed Sacrament','Demeurez devant le Saint-Sacrement')}</h2><p>${t('Do not fill the whole period with text. Scripture, prayer and sacred silence all belong to Eucharistic worship. Follow the actual public service being celebrated.','Ne remplissez pas tout le temps de textes. L’Écriture, la prière et le silence sacré ont leur place dans l’adoration eucharistique. Suivez le service public réellement célébré.')}</p>`;else body=`<div class="pbKicker">${esc(prayerTitle(p))}</div>${flipText(p.la,p[lang()]||p.en,'ben',false)}`;return `<div class="pbShell"><div class="pbFlow">${top(t('Benediction prayers','Prières de la bénédiction'))}<div class="pbProgress"><span style="width:${((i+1)/seq.length*100).toFixed(1)}%"></span></div><section class="pbFlowCard">${body}</section><nav class="pbFlowNav"><button data-pb-ben-prev ${i===0?'disabled':''}>← ${t('Previous','Précédent')}</button><button data-pb-ben-next>${i===seq.length-1?t('Finish','Terminer'):t('Next','Suivant')} →</button></nav><div class="pbSource">${t('This is a devotional companion to common traditional Benediction texts, not a replacement for the approved order actually being celebrated by the priest or deacon.','Ceci est un compagnon dévotionnel pour les textes traditionnels courants de la bénédiction, non un remplacement de l’ordre approuvé réellement célébré par le prêtre ou le diacre.')}</div></div></div>`}
function render(){let h='';if(S.view==='hub')h=renderHub();else if(S.view==='prayer')h=renderPrayer();else if(S.view==='confession')h=renderConfession();else if(S.view==='rosary')h=renderRosary();else if(S.view==='angelus')h=renderAngelus();else if(S.view==='stations')h=renderStations();else if(S.view==='benediction')h=renderBenediction();root.innerHTML=h;bind();queueMicrotask(()=>{globalThis.AO_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_V357?.compact?.();globalThis.AO_ROSARY_ART_V38?.sync?.();globalThis.AO_PRAYER_FOCUS_V392?.process?.();globalThis.AO_DEVOTIONAL_GESTURES_V393?.process?.();globalThis.AO_V411_ROLE_GESTURE_HARMONIZATION?.sync?.()})}
function launchExisting(action){const ret=externalReturn||{surface:'prayerbook',view:'hub'};close({silent:true});if(window.AO_NAV_V362?.openCore?.(action,ret))return true;const store=window.AO_RUNTIME_V8?.store;if(action==='prepare'){store?.dispatch({type:'enter-prepare'});return true}if(action==='thanks'){store?.dispatch({type:'enter-thanksgiving'});return true}return false}
function bind(){root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-back]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=back);root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-prayer]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.prayer=b.dataset.pbPrayer;go('prayer')});root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-module]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const m=b.dataset.pbModule;if(m==='confession')S.confStep=0;if(m==='rosary'){S.rosarySet=null;S.rosaryStep=0}if(m==='stations')S.station=0;if(m==='benediction')S.benedictionStep=0;go(m)});root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-existing]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>launchExisting(b.dataset.pbExisting));const q=root.querySelector('[data-pb-search]');if(q)q.oninput=e=>{S.query=e.target.value;const pos=e.target.selectionStart;root.innerHTML=renderHub();bind();queueMicrotask(()=>globalThis.AO_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_V357?.compact?.());const nq=root.querySelector('[data-pb-search]');nq.focus();nq.setSelectionRange(pos,pos)};root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-flip]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const lat=b.querySelector('[data-pb-latin]'),ver=b.querySelector('[data-pb-vern]');if(!lat||!ver)return;const was=b.dataset.face==='vernacular';b.dataset.face=was?'latin':'vernacular';lat.hidden=!was;ver.hidden=was;const h=b.nextElementSibling?.matches?.('[data-pb-flip-hint]')?b.nextElementSibling:null;if(h)h.textContent=was?t('Tap the text · show English','Touchez le texte · afficher le français'):t('Tap again · return to Latin','Touchez encore · retour au latin')});
const cp=root.querySelector('[data-pb-conf-prev]'),cn=root.querySelector('[data-pb-conf-next]');if(cp)cp.onclick=()=>{S.confStep=Math.max(0,S.confStep-1);render()};if(cn)cn.onclick=()=>{if(S.confStep>=7)return go('hub');S.confStep++;render()};root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-rosary-set]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.rosarySet=b.dataset.pbRosarySet;S.rosaryStep=0;render()});const rp=root.querySelector('[data-pb-rosary-prev]'),rn=root.querySelector('[data-pb-rosary-next]');if(rp)rp.onclick=()=>{S.rosaryStep=Math.max(0,S.rosaryStep-1);render()};if(rn)rn.onclick=()=>{if(S.rosaryStep>=6){S.rosarySet=null;S.rosaryStep=0;return render()}S.rosaryStep++;render()};const rc=root.querySelector('[data-pb-rosary-change]');if(rc)rc.onclick=()=>{S.rosarySet=null;S.rosaryStep=0;render()};root.querySelectorAll('[data-pb-station-mode]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.stationMode=b.dataset.pbStationMode;render()});const sp=root.querySelector('[data-pb-station-prev]'),sn=root.querySelector('[data-pb-station-next]');if(sp)sp.onclick=()=>{S.station=Math.max(0,S.station-1);render()};if(sn)sn.onclick=()=>{if(S.station>=14)return go('hub');S.station++;render()};const bp=root.querySelector('[data-pb-ben-prev]'),bn=root.querySelector('[data-pb-ben-next]');if(bp)bp.onclick=()=>{S.benedictionStep=Math.max(0,S.benedictionStep-1);render()};if(bn)bn.onclick=()=>{if(S.benedictionStep>=5)return go('hub');S.benedictionStep++;render()};const ac=root.querySelector('[data-pb-angelus-conclusion]');if(ac)ac.onchange=()=>{S.angelusConclusion=ac.checked;render()};const ta=root.querySelector('[data-pb-toggle-angelus]');if(ta)ta.onclick=()=>{const shown=S.angelusOverride?S.angelusOverride==='regina':paschal();S.angelusOverride=shown?'angelus':'regina';render()}}
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&root.classList.contains('open'))close()});

function aoFormation(titleEn,titleFr,body){return `<details class="pbFormation"><summary>${t(titleEn,titleFr)}</summary><div class="pbFormationBody">${body}</div></details>`}
function aoMysteryId(set,n){return ({joyful:'joy',sorrowful:'sor',glorious:'glo',luminous:'lum'}[set]||'joy')+(n+1)}
function aoMysteryFormation(set,n){
 const id=aoMysteryId(set,n),d=AO_ROSARY_DEPTH[id]; if(!d)return'';
 const isfr=lang()==='fr';
 const desc=isfr?d.description_fr:d.description,fruit=isfr?d.fruit_fr:d.fruit,com=isfr?d.commentary_fr:d.commentary_en;
 const cues=d.cues.map(c=>`<div class="pbCue"><b>${c.bead} · ${esc(c.reference)}</b>${isfr?'':`<span>${esc(c.excerpt)}</span>`}</div>`).join('');
 return `<div class="pbRosaryFormation"><div class="pbKicker">${t('Contemplate before the decade','Contempler avant la dizaine')}</div><p><strong>${esc(d.primary_passage)}</strong></p><p>${esc(desc)}</p><span class="pbFruit">${t('Fruit / grace: ','Fruit / grâce : ')}${esc(fruit)}</span><h3>${t('Traditional commentary','Commentaire traditionnel')}</h3><p>${esc(com)}</p><div class="pbSourceRow"><a href="${esc(d.source_url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(d.source_label)} ↗</a><a href="${esc(d.traditional_commentary_url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(d.traditional_commentary_label)} ↗</a></div><details class="pbScriptureCues"><summary>${t('Scripture through the ten Hail Marys','Repères bibliques pour les dix Ave Maria')}</summary><div class="pbCueList">${cues}</div>${isfr?`<p class="pbTiny">${t('','Les références proviennent du jeu de 200 repères scripturaires vérifiés d’Ad Orientem. Les extraits bibliques anglais Douay-Rheims sont affichés en mode EN ; le mode FR n’invente pas ici une traduction biblique non sourcée.')}</p>`:''}</details></div>`;
}
function aoRosaryPracticeIntro(){return aoFormation('How the traditional Rosary is prayed','Comment prier le Rosaire traditionnel',`
<h3>${t('What is essential','Structure')}</h3><p>${t('Leo XIII describes the Rosary as two inseparable elements: meditation on the mysteries and vocal prayer. The traditional Dominican/Roman cycle has fifteen mysteries — Joyful, Sorrowful and Glorious — corresponding symbolically to the 150 Psalms.','Léon XIII décrit le Rosaire comme deux éléments inséparables : la méditation des mystères et la prière vocale. Le cycle dominicain/romain traditionnel comporte quinze mystères — joyeux, douloureux et glorieux — correspondant symboliquement aux 150 Psaumes.')}</p>
<h3>${t('Opening and closing','Ouverture et conclusion')}</h3><p>${t('The Creed, introductory Pater, three Aves and Gloria are a widespread traditional opening, but local prayer books have varied. Salve Regina and the Rosary collect are customary conclusions; the Litany of Loreto may be added but is not required to make five decades a Rosary.','Le Credo, le Pater d’introduction, les trois Ave et le Gloria constituent une ouverture traditionnelle très répandue, mais les livres de prières ont varié. Le Salve Regina et l’oraison du Rosaire sont des conclusions usuelles ; les Litanies de Lorette peuvent s’ajouter sans être nécessaires pour que les cinq dizaines constituent un chapelet.')}</p>
<h3>${t('Contemplation','Contemplation')}</h3><p>${t('The mystery is announced before each decade. John Paul II later recommended a biblical passage and a brief silence; this is a modern pastoral articulation of the Rosary’s older contemplative purpose, not a replacement of the traditional prayers.','Le mystère est annoncé avant chaque dizaine. Jean-Paul II a ensuite recommandé un passage biblique et un bref silence ; c’est une explicitation pastorale moderne de la finalité contemplative plus ancienne du Rosaire, non un remplacement des prières traditionnelles.')}</p>
<div class="pbSourceRow"><a href="${AO_LINKS.rosaryLeo}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Leo XIII · Iucunda Semper ↗</a><a href="${AO_LINKS.rosaryRVM}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Rosarium Virginis Mariae §§19, 29–31 ↗</a></div>`)}

const _aoRenderRosary=renderRosary;
renderRosary=function(){
 if(!S.rosarySet){
  const sug=suggestedMystery();
